## Research notes by topic

The researchers' notes, merged: W work route, scams and document checks; L life and problems; B Bangladesh side, money home, coming home, corridor numbers; V short stays; S student, visa change, asylum, children; E domestic workers, family, long stay; M the user's manual files; A, D, F, H, N practical pass; R re-check of every lead and conflict; X gap research; C coverage check.

Merged from the per-topic notes in `findings-parts/`. Ledger ids like [W0012] point to rows in ledger.csv.


## W: W

## Findings, part W: Work visa (company worker) to Qatar, Bangladesh to residence permit, plus scams and document checks

Researched 7 Nov 2026. Ledger ids are in `ledger-parts/W.csv` (W0001-W0106). The one Bangla image PDF (BMET permission letter) was read page by page at 2.5x and 5x; a transcription is saved as `raw/bmet-qatar-permission-bts-rl2434-2025-09.transcription.md` and those rows carry `--no-quote-check` with a note. Qatar laws are quoted from Al Meezan (Ministry of Justice legal portal, status "In Force" on 7 Nov 2026); the ledger tool forces "possibly-outdated" on them because the publish date is 2004/2015, but they are the law in force.

Key terms (explain inline in the doc): **MOL** = Qatar Ministry of Labour (formerly ADLSA, Ministry of Administrative Development, Labour and Social Affairs; old URLs say adlsa.gov.qa). **MOI** = Qatar Ministry of Interior (issues visas and residence permits). **QVC** = Qatar Visa Center, run in Bangladesh by SQ Services Limited under MOI's mandate, where the worker gives biometrics, signs the contract and has the medical. **RP** = residence permit; **QID** = Qatar ID card that carries it. **Metrash** = MOI's mobile app. **Hukoomi** = Qatar government services portal. **PRO / mandoob** = the company's government-relations clerk. **Labour wing** = labour section of the Bangladesh Embassy in Doha, which attests demand letters and contracts. **OEP** = BMET's Overseas Employment Platform. **BOESL** = Bangladesh Overseas Employment and Services Ltd (state recruiter).

### 1. Is Qatar open to Bangladeshi workers? (Nov 2026)

- **Yes, and it is the third-largest destination this year.** BMET cleared 53,611 workers for Qatar from 1 Jan to 6 Nov 2026, after Saudi Arabia and Singapore [W0051], and 107,598 in 2025 [W0001].
- **No Qatari ban on Bangladeshis was found.** In Oct 2025 the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare called "Qatar has stopped visas for Bangladeshis" posts false and said the Qatari labour market "remains open and fully operational" [W0002, lead: news report of the Ministry statement; the statement itself is not on probashi.gov.bd]. It also said there is no "free visa" in Qatar's visa system [W0003, lead].
- **Quotas:** no nationality quota found. The employer's MOL approval sets gender, number and profession, then a "nationalities selection system" step follows [W0023]. **Need to know:** whether any nationality cap applies to Bangladeshis per company or sector (searched MOL/Hukoomi, Embassy Doha, BMET; nothing found).
- **Sector interest 2026:** at the 7th Bangladesh-Qatar Joint Committee (Dhaka, 18 May 2026) Qatar said it wants electricians, plumbers, AC technicians and welders trained at five designated TTCs [W0007, W0008, lead]. Qatar's labour minister put the number of Bangladeshis employed in Qatar at about 473,000 [W0010, lead]. The Embassy says Qatar has taken Bangladeshi workers since 1976 [W0012].
- **Labour agreement:** an MoU on cooperation in the field of labour was signed during the Amir's visit, 23 Apr 2024 [W0011, lead]. Text not online (blocked list). The Joint Committee on Labour meets regularly (7th meeting May 2026) [W0106, lead].
- **G2G / BOESL:** BOESL's notice page has nothing on Qatar (searched Qatar and কাতার) [W0006]. No G2G channel for Qatar found.
- **BMET permission letters:** BMET's Miscellaneous information page has only one Qatar letter (100 women housemaids, BTS Overseas RL-2434, published 24 Sep 2025) [W0004]; the English search "Qatar" returns nothing [W0005]. So, unlike Saudi Arabia, a worker going to a Qatar company job usually will **not** find a BMET permission letter to check. **Need to know:** whether BMET issues (but doesn't post) permission letters for Qatar male/company job orders, and what replaces it (BMET clearance on the attested demand letter is the visible control) (searched bmet.gov.bd misc-infos in English and Bangla, archived view, agency-permission category list of ~40 recent letters: Saudi, Japan, Malaysia, Libya, Russia, Cambodia, one Qatar).

### 2. ILO fair-recruitment pilot Bangladesh-Qatar (ILO is official for its own programmes)

- 2018-2021 pilot in Qatar's construction sector, run by MOL (then ADLSA) and the ILO Project Office for Qatar: a Qatari sub-contractor and a Bangladeshi private recruitment agency were audited against the ILO fair-recruitment principles and trained; the service agreement was amended; a grievance channel and pre-departure programme were built [W0013].
- Result: average migration cost fell 92%; about 93% of workers recruited after the pilot had no debt [W0014, W0015].
- The ILO brief does not name the companies. **Need to know:** names of the pilot partners from an official source. Leads only: a web summary names STS Group (Qatar) and Bonanza Overseas (Bangladesh) (search snippet, not saved; the ILO webinar page lists STS Group as a speaker, raw/ilo-bd-qatar-pilot-event, not ledgered). Bonanza (RL-0579) is the agency the Saudi doc already names on ILO evidence; confirm before naming it for Qatar.

### 3. Employer side (how a genuine Qatar work visa is created)

1. **Recruitment approval from MOL.** The company's authorised signatory logs in to MOL's Establishments Portal with Qatar's national login (NAS/Tawtheeq smart card) and files "Request to Issue a Labour Recruitment Approval"; free; the establishment ID must be active and not banned [W0023, W0024]. MOL staff decide; if approved (gender, number, profession), it goes to nationality selection and then to MOI [W0023]. A fast-track route caps a new company's first request at 10 visas [W0025]. Temporary work visa approvals are a separate route for government, energy and government-contract firms [W0026]. Employers can track MOL applications by application number, year and establishment ID (MOL 16505 / 44068979) [W0027].
2. **Visa at MOI.** "Work Visa on Company Sponsorship": fee QAR 300; requirements: attested education certificates for the profession, a home-country police clearance attested by the Qatar Embassy, an employment contract, and passing the medical outside Qatar [W0052, W0028, W0029, W0030].
3. **Contract entered in MOI's system.** For specified nationalities the employer enters contract details when issuing the visa, and the visa "cannot be printed or used" until the worker and employer have signed the contract; the employer signs and pays QR 490 through the "Examination Procedures outside Country" service [W0065, W0066, W0067].
4. **Law on recruiters and fees.** An employer may recruit from abroad only through a licensed recruiter or, for its own account, with MOL approval (Labour Law Art. 28) [W0016, W0017]. Recruiting for others needs a 2-year MOL licence (Art. 29) [W0018]. A licensed recruiter may not charge the worker "any amounts as fees, recruitment expenses, or any other costs" (Art. 33) [W0019]; penalty up to 1 month's jail and/or QR 2,000-6,000, plus closure/licence revocation [W0020]; since 2026, repeat violators can be named on MOL's website and fined administratively [W0021]. A recruiter's job ends at hand-over to the employer on arrival (Art. 34) [W0022].
5. **Residence law.** Work entry permits are granted only under a contract with the recruiter; visas may not be transferred, sold or traded, paid or unpaid (Law 21/2015 Art. 4) [W0031, W0032]; penalty up to 3 years' jail and/or QR 50,000 (repeat QR 20,000-100,000) [W0033]. The employer alone is responsible for the worker's residency (Art. 17) [W0037].
6. **Qatar's licensed recruitment offices:** MOL published a list of 221 registered recruitment companies in Sept 2026 [W0105, lead; official list blocked].
- **Need to know:** an explicit Qatari rule that the *employer* (not only a licensed recruiter) may not recover visa/recruitment costs from the worker (Art. 33 applies to licensees; searched Labour Law consolidated text and Law 9/2026; no employer-specific clause found in the parts read).

### 4. Bangladesh side, Qatar-specific

- **Demand letter attestation (Embassy Doha labour wing).** The Qatar company, its PRO or nominated agent submits the demand letter with (1) the agreement with the agent, (2) the Qatar government visa approval letter, (3) company details; Sun-Thu 7:30-14:00 [W0046, W0047, W0048]. Page dated 2021.
- **Contract attestation (individual male workers).** Sponsor or company PRO submits the contract with copies of the worker's passport and valid visa and company details; QR 50 per contract; ready after 3 working days [W0049, W0050]. Page dated 2021.
- **What a Qatar permission letter contains (the one example, women housemaids, 5 Aug 2025):** memo number and date; agency and RL number with address and mobile; employer name and Qatar address; the employer's demand reference "Ref. No: LW-QA-456, 03/06/2025"; job, number, salary 1000-1200 (currency not stated); 2-year contract, 3-month probation, 8 h/day, 48 h/week, max 12 h/day; ticket, food, medical, housing paid by the company; "migration cost: employer will bear" [W0038, W0039, W0040]. Conditions: workers from the BMET database only; contract signed before clearance; demand letter/power of attorney and skill certificates to BMET before the smart card; attested Bangla translation of the contract to each worker; employer pays all costs to arrival (levy, airfare); an agency representative flies with or meets each group at Doha airport; flight list to embassy and BMET; agency undertaking on Tk 300 stamp to pay food, lodging, return and compensation if things go wrong [W0041-W0045]. The "LW-QA-nnn" format looks like the labour wing's attestation number; that reading is ours, not stated (W0039 note).
- **Maximum cost for Qatar:** no official figure found. Reported Jan 2018: government re-fixed maximums for 16 countries, "Oman and Qatar - Tk 100,780" [W0104, unofficial]. In the 2025 women-worker letter, migration cost is borne by the employer [W0040].
- **Skill test:** none Qatar-specific found (no Qatari equivalent of Saudi Takamol/SVP). The education certificates must be attested for the visa [W0028]. **Need to know** (searched MOI/MOL/Hukoomi and BMET).
- **Police clearance:** required for the visa and must be attested by the Qatar Embassy [W0029]; get it via the generic Bangladesh-side SOP. QVC has a document attestation counter (attestation and Qatar Embassy legalisation via service providers) [W0059].
- **BMET clearance, OEP, PDO, Welfare Board:** generic part. Qatar-specific: BMET wants the demand letter/power of attorney (attested) and skill certificates before issuing the smart card [W0042].

### 5. QVC (Qatar Visa Center), Dhaka

- Operator and address: SQ Services Limited Bangladesh, under MOI's mandate; Rupayan Trade Center, 114 Kazi Nazrul Islam Avenue (11th floor), Bangla Motor, Ramna, Dhaka-1000; 09666 777 101 [W0056, W0057, W0058].
- What happens: book on the QVC website, attend, give biometrics, then medical at the centre [W0058]. MOI (as reported): booking uses the MOI reference number after the employer's initial approval [W0089, lead]; at the centre the reference and passport are validated, documents submitted, the contract shown for review and e-signature, then biometrics and medical; a copy of the contract is handed over [W0090, lead]; the worker then waits for the employer to tell them the result [W0091, lead]; when everything is passed the work visa is issued [W0092, lead]. MOI 2024: at QVC workers register biometrics, sign contracts and do medicals [W0093, lead]; drivers' vision tests count in Qatar [W0096, lead]; a temporary work visa is single-entry up to 12 months [W0095, lead].
- Walk-in allowed if you miss the slot or want an earlier time, but seats are limited [W0054, W0055]. Lounge is optional, paid, and does not speed up the visa [W0060].
- QVC's own warning: QVCs do not offer jobs; beware people posing as QVC agents [W0053].
- Status: MOI's Visa Services page links "Track Application Status (Qatar Visa Center)" to qatarvisacenter.com [W0061].
- Centres: Dhaka and Sylhet listed July 2024 [W0094, lead]; in May 2026 the Bangladesh minister said there is only one, in Dhaka, and asked for centres in all 8 divisions [W0009, lead]. Conflict (both news).
- **Need to know (QVC pages blocked):** documents to bring, the fee in Tk and who pays it, hours, how long results take, how the worker sees the result, what "unfit" means, re-test/appeal.

### 6. Arrival, medical in Qatar, residence permit

- Within 30 days of entry: medical check, then fingerprints, then residency procedures [W0078]. Medical Commission: original passport, copy with phone number, and the medical certificate from the accredited centre at home; QR 100 by card; result goes electronically to MOI [W0076, W0077].
- The employer issues the RP online ("Issue/Reactivate RP by Visa Number"); the worker must first do medical and fingerprints; for companies the QID card comes by Qatar Post [W0074, W0075]. Employer is responsible for residency [W0037]; RP renewal within 90 days of expiry, late fine QR 10/day up to QR 6,000 [W0079].
- Passport: the employer must return it after RP procedures unless the worker asks in writing for it to be kept; fine up to QR 25,000 [W0034, W0035].
- Can't find your company on arrival: take visa and passport copies to MOL's Labour Relations Department or the nearest police station; CID Search and Follow-up or Public Prosecution residency affairs can give the employer's number; or ask the labour wing [W0083]. QID not issued: ask the company PRO/manager/HR, then complain at Public Prosecution residency affairs (inside the CID office) with visa and passport [W0084, W0085] (Embassy FAQ 2021).
- Contract: in Arabic, translation may be attached, Arabic prevails [W0072]; written, authenticated, three copies, states employer, worker, date, work, start, duration, wage [W0073, W0071]. After arrival get the authenticated contract from MOL's contract printing portal (QID or visa number + mobile + SMS code; free; 16505/44068979) [W0070]; get a hard copy from the company too because it is often not online [W0086].
- Who pays what (official so far): MOI visa fee QAR 300 and QVC contract service QR 490 are employer-side MOI account fees [W0052, W0066]; licensed recruiters cannot charge workers [W0019]; employer bears costs to arrival in the BMET letter [W0043]. **Need to know:** who pays the QVC medical/biometric fee and the Qatar Medical Commission QR 100 for company workers; who pays the airfare for company (male) workers under Qatari law.

### 7. Scams

Done to workers:
- **Free visa / visa trading.** Illegal in Qatar: visas may not be sold or transferred [W0032]; up to 3 years' jail and QR 50,000 [W0033]; Embassy: buying/selling visas and working on a "free visa" are both illegal, and a free-visa holder has no legal help if unpaid [W0080, W0081, W0082]. Ministry: no "free visa" exists in Qatar [W0003, lead]. In practice: Qatar employers/agents demand USD 300-500 per visa from Bangladeshi agents, passed to workers [W0098, unofficial]; some agencies buy visas for QR 5,000-6,000 [W0099, unofficial].
- **Fake companies.** MOI arrested nine people in 2023 for setting up fake companies to trade visas [W0097, unofficial].
- **Fees.** Illegal for licensed recruiters [W0019]; Bangladeshi workers typically paid USD 3,000-4,000 [W0103, unofficial]; Guardian estimate USD 1.5-2 bn in 2011-2020 [W0100, unofficial].
- **Contract substitution.** Case: promised welder at QR 2,500, put on a desert farm at QR 800 [W0102, unofficial]; QVC contract signing may have reduced substitution but not fees [W0101, unofficial]. Defence: read and keep the contract signed at QVC [W0090, lead]; check it on MOL's portal after arrival [W0070].
- **Fake QVC agents / job offers.** QVC offers no jobs [W0053].
- **Unlicensed recruitment offices in Qatar.** MOL's 221-company list [W0105, lead].
Tried by applicants:
- False information or documents: the RP is revoked (Law 21/2015 Art. 37) [W0036]. Biometrics are taken at QVC [W0058]. **Need to know:** an official Qatar or Bangladesh warning specific to fake age, fake certificates or a borrowed identity at QVC (searched MOI, Hukoomi, Embassy Doha, BMET; none found).

### Draft steps (with the how)

**Step 1: Find a real job.**
1. Register on OEP (generic SOP). Have a trade; Qatar has asked for electricians, plumbers, AC technicians and welders from five TTCs [W0007, W0008, lead]; ask BMET/your TTC which five (Need to know).
2. A relative's company: the company's PRO gets MOL recruitment approval [W0023] and the MOI visa [W0052], then gets the demand letter attested at the Embassy Doha with the agent agreement, the visa approval letter and company details [W0046, W0047]. Nobody should pay for the visa [W0019, W0032].
3. A licensed Bangladeshi agency: check the licence on OEP (SOP 1) [W0087]; ask for the attested demand letter and the Qatar visa approval letter [W0047].
4. Avoid free visas [W0080-W0082].

**Step 2: Before paying.** Get the employer name, job, salary, contract terms in writing; the contract must state them [W0073]. Check the visa on MOI (SOP 3) [W0063]. Pay by bank; record payments (generic). Official maximum for Qatar: Need to know; reported Tk 100,780 (2018) [W0104, unofficial]; in the one 2025 BMET letter the employer bore migration cost [W0040].

**Step 3: Police clearance and attested certificates.** Police clearance (generic SOP), attested by MOFA and the Qatar Embassy; attested education certificates [W0028, W0029]; QVC attestation counter [W0059].

**Step 4: QVC: contract, biometrics, medical.** Book on qatarvisacenter.com with the MOI reference number [W0058, W0089]; attend Rupayan Trade Center [W0056]; read the contract before e-signing; keep your copy [W0090]; walk-in possible [W0054]; wait for the employer to tell you the result [W0091]; track via MOI link [W0061].

**Step 5: Visa issued; BMET clearance; flight.** Visa usable only after contract signed [W0065]. BMET clearance with attested demand letter/power of attorney and skill certificates; attested Bangla contract translation [W0042]. Employer pays costs to arrival in BMET's letter [W0043]; agency representative meets workers [W0044].

**Step 6: Arrival and RP.** Medical within 30 days, fingerprints, RP [W0078, W0076]; employer issues RP, card by Qatar Post [W0074, W0075]; passport returned [W0034]; check RP and contract (SOPs 6, 7).

### Draft SOPs

**SOP W1: Check a Bangladeshi agency's licence (OEP).**
- When: before talking money with any agency.
- Steps: open https://www.oep.gov.bd/agencies; type the RL number or name in Search; read the row: licence number, name, address, office phone, licence status, validity date [W0087]. Call the office phone shown there, not a broker's number (generic).
- What you see: status Active / Expired / Cancelled / Suspended (on 7 Nov 2026: 2,205 Active, 264 Expired, 235 Cancelled, 151 Suspended of 2,863) [W0087]. Example: RL2434 BTS OVERSEAS LIMITED, Active [W0088].
- If wrong: complaint route (generic part).

**SOP W2: Look for BMET's permission letter for a Qatar job.**
- Steps: https://bmet.gov.bd/pages/miscellaneous-infos, search কাতার (Bangla) [W0004]; English "Qatar" returns nothing [W0005].
- What you see: for company jobs, usually nothing; only one Qatar letter (women domestic workers, 2025) [W0004]. A real letter shows memo no., agency RL, employer, demand ref (e.g. LW-QA-456), job, pay, conditions, who bears migration cost [W0038-W0040].
- If absent: rely on SOPs W3-W5. Need to know: BMET's Qatar practice.

**SOP W3: Check the Qatar visa is real (MOI Visa Inquiry).**
- Steps: MOI Inquiries > Visa Services > Visa Inquiry (https://portal.moi.gov.qa/wps/portal/MOIInternet/services/inquiries/visaservices, form at https://fees2.moi.gov.qa/moipay/visa/inquiry?language=en); enter visa number or passport number, select nationality, Submit [W0062, W0063, W0064]. Also in Metrash (Need to know: exact menu).
- What you see: latest status; if rejected, the Remarks icon shows the reason; issued visas can be printed [W0063]. Check the visa type (work), the employer's name and the job.
- If wrong: do not pay; complain (generic). Need to know: what fields the result shows (form not viewable).

**SOP W4: Check the demand letter was attested.**
- Steps: ask the agency for the attested demand letter and the Qatar visa approval letter it was attested with [W0047]. Need to know: whether the Embassy or BMET lets a worker verify an attestation number (e.g. "LW-QA-456") [W0039]; Embassy pages are from 2021.

**SOP W5: Check the employer exists.** Need to know: MOCI public CR lookup (no official worker-usable page found; Hukoomi lists MOCI services for companies only; third-party sites claim a MOCI search). Interim: MOL approval requires an active, unbanned establishment ID [W0024]; ask the company for its establishment ID and CR number.

**SOP W6: QVC appointment and result.**
- Steps: book at qatarvisacenter.com (choose Bangladesh) with the MOI reference number [W0058, W0089]; go to Rupayan Trade Center 11th floor, Bangla Motor; phone 09666 777 101 [W0056]; missed slot: walk-in [W0054]; track status via MOI's link "Track Application Status (Qatar Visa Center)" [W0061].
- What you see: contract on screen for review and e-signature, copy given [W0090]; result told by employer [W0091].
- If something goes wrong: QVC offers no jobs; anyone selling QVC slots or jobs is not QVC [W0053]. Need to know: fees, documents, unfit/appeal.

**SOP W7: Check your contract and RP after arrival.**
- Contract: Hukoomi "Portal for Printing Employment Contracts": enter QID or visa number and mobile, then the SMS code; free; MOL 16505/44068979 [W0070]; compare with the QVC copy.
- RP application: Hukoomi/MOI "Residence Permit Application Tracking" needs visa number + the sponsor's ID number + code [W0068] (so ask the PRO). Once you have a QID: "Inquire about the Expiry Date of Official Documents" by ID number, or by passport number + nationality [W0069].
- If no QID: ask PRO/manager/HR, then complain at Public Prosecution residency affairs with visa and passport [W0084, W0085]; employer had to start medical/fingerprints within 30 days [W0078].

**SOP W8: Report visa trading / free-visa offers.** Law: Art. 4 and 38 [W0032, W0033]; Embassy warning [W0080]. Bangladesh side: BMET/Ministry complaint (generic). Need to know: a Qatar MOI reporting channel for visa trading usable by workers (MOI Search and Follow-up Department arrests reported [W0097, unofficial]).

### Need to know (with what was searched)
- QVC: documents, fee (Tk) and who pays, hours, time to result, how to see result, unfit criteria, re-test/appeal; whether Sylhet still operates (qatarvisacenter.com blocked; Wayback empty shell; Firecrawl only country page; news only).
- Official maximum migration cost for Qatar (probashi.gov.bd notices/press search for কাতার empty; BMET notices; only 2018 news, W0104).
- BMET permission-letter practice for Qatar company jobs (BMET misc-infos Bangla/English, category list).
- Which five TTCs and how to apply for the Qatar skilled-trade intake (news only, W0007).
- Bangladesh-Qatar 2024 labour MoU text (news only, W0011).
- Who pays QVC medical, Qatar Medical Commission QR 100 and airfare for company workers under Qatari law (labour law sections read: 28-38; not found).
- Public commercial-registration check for Qatar employers (Hukoomi MOCI services, moci.gov.qa search; none for the public found).
- Whether workers can verify an Embassy demand-letter attestation (Embassy Doha pages 2021; notices page has 3 unrelated items).
- Skill test for Qatar (none found on MOI/MOL/Hukoomi/BMET).
- Official warning on fake age/documents at QVC.
- Nationality quotas for Bangladeshis.

### Conflicts
- W0094 (Gulf Times, July 2024: QVCs in Dhaka and Sylhet) vs W0009 (Bangladesh Monitor, May 2026: "only one Qatar visa and medical center in Dhaka"). Both news; the 2026 report is newer.
- W0040 / W0043 (BMET 2025: employer bears migration cost) vs W0104 (2018 news: maximum Tk 100,780 a worker may pay for Qatar). Different years and job types (domestic vs general); not strictly a conflict but both must be shown.
- Embassy FAQ (2021) uses the old ministry name ADLSA and address empcont.adlsa.gov.qa for contracts; Hukoomi 2026 page (W0070) calls it MOL's contract printing portal. Use the 2026 page.
