See `blocked-parts/W.md`: qatarvisacenter.com Bangladesh pages (how to apply, documents, fees, FAQ, track); mol.gov.qa licensed recruitment offices list and recruitment-approval service; Qatar Embassy Kathmandu 2019 QVC note; Ministry statements of 21 Oct 2025 and 18 May 2026 (Facebook screenshots); 2017 migration-cost notification; 2024 Bangladesh-Qatar labour MoU.


## L: L

## Findings, part L: life and problems in Qatar on a residence permit, and getting home

Researcher L, 7 November 2026. Ledger: `ledger-parts/L.csv` (L0001-L0228; facts in `L.facts.csv`). Blocked: `blocked-parts/L.md`.

**How the law was read.** Al Meezan (almeezan.qa, the Ministry of Justice's legal portal) is now a JavaScript app; the old `LawPage.aspx` links redirect to the home page. Its public API (`https://api.moj.gov.qa/api/v1/Legislation/published-lookup?searchTerm=...` and `/Legislation/<id>?lang=1`) returns the full consolidated text, with each article's current version. I saved the JSON (`raw/almeezan-api-*.json`) and a markdown text copy built only from the API fields (`raw/almeezan-api-*.md`, SOURCE line gives the human URL `https://almeezan.qa/legislations/<id>`). Many articles exist only in Arabic on the portal, so those quotes are Arabic; Law 9/2026's English is the portal's machine translation (flag set in the API; noted in each row). The ledger tool forces "possibly-outdated" on laws dated before Nov 2025, but every law used was shown "In Force" on 7 Nov 2026.

**Big new finding: Law No. 9 of 2026** (published 11 June 2026) amended the Labour Law: a new compulsory dispute route (Art. 115 bis), a worker's right to change employer or quit with the ticket paid when wages are not paid (Art. 146 bis), up to 1 year prison for wage-payment breaches (Art. 145 bis), new strike rules, training/test certificate for some trades. And **Minister of Labour Decision 50/2026** (1 Sep 2026): wages due on the 1st, paid through WPS within 7 days. **Council of Ministers Decision 35/2026** (2 Sep 2026) reshaped the dispute committees. None of these appear in news-based guides yet.

Terms to explain inline in the doc: **MOL** Ministry of Labour (before 2021 "ADLSA/MADLSA", Ministry of Administrative Development, Labour and Social Affairs). **MOI** Ministry of Interior. **QID** Qatar ID card (residence permit, RP). **Metrash** MOI app. **WPS** Wage Protection System (salary by bank transfer, monitored by MOL). **WSIF** Workers' Support and Insurance Fund. **Labour Dispute Settlement Committee** (court-like body chaired by a judge, at MOL). **Search and Follow-up Department** (MOI, Salwa Road / CID building; absconding, surrender, deportation). **NAS / Tawtheeq** National Authentication System login. **Mandoob / PRO** the company's government-relations clerk. **NHRC** National Human Rights Committee.

---

### 1. After arrival (QID, medical, Metrash, passport, bank, SIM, health card)

Part W covers the arrival medical, fingerprints and RP issue in detail (W0074-W0079); not repeated here.

- **Residence permit timing.** Within 30 days of entry the employer must let the worker go to the authorities for the RP, and the worker may not refuse [L0101]. The sponsor must renew it within 90 days of expiry [L0100]. Late steps are an offence with a settlement of QR 10 a day up to QR 6,000 [L0121, L0116].
- **MOI fees and papers (undated MOI pages, "© 2021").** First RP on company sponsorship: attested labour contract, signed form, original passport, company establishment ID copy, photo, blood group certificate [L0137]; RP QR 1,000 a year + QR 100 ID card, work permit QR 100 a year [L0138]. Renewal at Expatriates Affairs, MOI service centres, Metrash2 or MOI e-services [L0139]. Moving the RP to a new passport QR 100 [L0141]. These are employer-side fees. **Need to know:** whether the worker may be charged any of them (Labour Law Art. 33 bans recruitment offices charging workers [L0220]; no article found that says who pays RP fees).
- **Metrash.** MOI's free app: 400+ services (residence, visas, traffic), e-wallet of official documents, in Arabic, English, French, Spanish, Hindi and Urdu, not Bangla [L0147, L0148]. MOI services pages name "Metrash2" for leave notification, RP renewal/cancellation and absconding reports [L0142, L0139, L0140, L0143]. **Need to know:** official registration steps for the new Metrash app (searched portal.moi.gov.qa services, App Store listing, web; only third-party guides say: QID + SIM registered under your QID + passcode). The embassy FAQ (2021) says a lost QID is replaced via Metrash by the company PRO, or at a Jawazat service centre with original passport + QID copy [L0171].
- **Passport.** The employer must hand the passport back once RP procedures are done unless the worker asks in writing for it to be kept, and must give it back on request [L0098]; fine up to QR 25,000 [L0099, L0218]. The worker must show passport/RP when asked and report a loss immediately [L0106]; lost passport: police GD (QR 10) then the embassy [L0184, L0177].
- **Bank account.** Wages must be transferred to the worker's account at a financial institution in Qatar [L0019] through WPS within 7 days of the 1st of the month [L0088]. At one bank (QIB) the employer starts the salary account on the bank's WPS portal; the worker needs a valid QID, passport and local mobile; for earners up to QAR 5,000; free debit card, no minimum balance [L0207, L0208, unofficial: bank's product page]. **Need to know:** a Qatar Central Bank rule on worker accounts (qcb.gov.qa reachable but no worker-account page found in time).
- **SIM.** **Need to know:** official Ooredoo/Vodafone ID rules (only third-party guides: QID for residents, passport for visitors). Metrash registration reportedly needs a SIM in your own name; the MOL change-employer service needs "a mobile number registered in their name" [L0185, lead].
- **Health card.** At the nearest PHCC centre: valid QID, 4x3 cm photo, utility bill as proof of address, QR 100 by card only (no cash) [L0149, L0150]; gives subsidised care at PHCC and Hamad hospitals and government pharmacies [L0151]; renew at PHCC/HMC or online via Hukoomi; replacement QR 100 (valid) / QR 200 (expired) [L0152, L0153].
- **Health insurance.** Law 22/2021 makes health insurance compulsory for migrants and visitors [L0132]; the employer pays premiums, gives the card, and may not charge workers [L0134]; no RP issue/renewal without proof of cover [L0133]; emergency care without payment [L0135]; exemptions incl. temporary-RP holders [L0136]. **Need to know:** whether the residents' phase is actually in force in Nov 2026 (GCO only says the Shura approved the draft in 2021; news says only visitors since 2023). Searched almeezan (insurance decisions list: only visitor premium decisions 17/2022, 3/2023), GCO page.

### 2. Rights at work

- **Who is covered.** The Labour Law does not cover domestic workers, casual, agricultural, part-time or freelance workers unless the Cabinet extends it [L0070] (domestic workers: Law 15/2017, part W/other part).
- **Contract.** Written, MOL-authenticated, three copies [L0001]. Probation max 6 months, once per employer [L0002].
- **Minimum wage.** QR 1,000 basic + QR 500 housing + QR 300 food if not provided [L0074, L0211]; in force since March 2021 [L0075, L0211]; no agreement below it is valid, whatever the skill [L0076]; reviewed at least yearly [L0077]. No later decision changing the amounts on Al Meezan (searched 7 Nov 2026).
- **Pay.** Due on the 1st, paid through WPS into a Qatari account within 7 days [L0088]; QAR only [L0019]. Deductions: loans max 10% a month, no interest; total max 50% [L0021, L0022]. No forced buying from company shops [L0023]. No charge for safety gear [L0043] or housing [L0087]. Recruitment offices may not charge workers any fees [L0220].
- **Hours.** 48 a week / 8 a day; Ramadan 36/6; commuting not counted [L0024, L0025]; breaks 1-3 h, max 5 h in a row [L0026]; total with overtime max 10 h a day [L0027]. Overtime +25% [L0028]; 9 pm-3 am +50% (not shift workers) [L0029]. Weekly paid rest 24 h, normally Friday; working it = another rest day + rest-day pay or +150%; max 2 Fridays in a row [L0030, L0031].
- **Holidays and leave.** Eid al-Fitr 3 days, Eid al-Adha 3 days, National Day 1 day (Law 9/2026), 3 days chosen by employer [L0032, L0033]. Annual leave 3 weeks (<5 years), 4 weeks (5+ years), pro rata [L0034]; leave pay before leave [L0035]; untaken leave paid in cash at contract end [L0036]. Sick leave after 3 months' service with an employer-approved doctor's certificate; 2 weeks full pay, 4 weeks half pay, then unpaid; contract can end after 12 weeks [L0037, L0038]. Hajj: up to 20 days unpaid, once [L0039]. No dismissal notice during leave [L0040].
- **Heat.** 1 June-15 Sept no outdoor/unshaded work 10:00-15:30 [L0078]; free cool water, shade, light clothing, free yearly checks [L0079]; stop work above WBGT 32.1 [L0080]; worker may stop and complain to MOL, no dismissal for it [L0081]; schedule posted [L0082].
- **Housing (MD 18/2014).** 6 m2 per bed; no corridors/roofs/basements [L0083]; bed, lockable storage for documents, AC [L0084, L0085]; one bathroom per 6 workers [L0086]; no deductions [L0087]; employer-provided housing must meet these [L0044].
- **Injury.** Employer reports to police and MOL at once [L0045]; treatment at employer's cost; full pay up to 6 months then half [L0046]; compensation for death (Islamic-law diya) or permanent disability [L0047]; exclusions (drugs/alcohol, wilful) [L0048]; claim lapses 1 year after final medical report or death [L0049]; employer pays disability compensation in 15 days, death compensation to court in 15 days [L0050]; medical disputes go to the medical body [L0051].
- **Strikes.** Allowed only under strict conditions (3/4 vote, 2 weeks' notice, MOL approval with MOI, max 6 days, not in vital sectors, unpaid) [L0071]; inciting an unlawful strike is a dismissal ground [L0072].
- **Trades.** Some professions need an MOL-accredited training/test certificate (list on MOL website) [L0073].
- **End-of-service gratuity.** At least 3 weeks' basic wage per year after 1 year, pro rata, on last basic wage; employer may deduct debts [L0013, L0014]. **Note:** Saudi/older guides say "21 days"; the law says three weeks.

### 3. How long you can stay

- RP is tied to the employer; renewed by the sponsor [L0100]. Fixed-term contracts last at most 5 years, renewable [L0226]. **Need to know:** the usual RP validity for company workers (MOI fees are per year, with 20% off for a 3-year renewal [L0227]).
- After the RP expires or is cancelled, you must leave within 90 days [L0111].
- Outside Qatar more than 6 months in a row: RP lapses unless return permission obtained [L0105].
- No permanent residence for ordinary workers: permanent residence (Law 10/2018) is for narrow categories (part E/long-stay). Family residence: spouse, sons studying up to 25, unmarried daughters [L0124]; MOI conditions (salary QR 10,000, or QR 6,000 with family housing, technical/specialised job) are in `qna-moi-family-visas-2023-12` (other part's raw; cite their row).

### 4. What can get you jailed or deported

- **Leaving your employer / working for someone else / overstaying / visa trading:** up to 3 years and/or QR 50,000 [L0114, L0115, L0107]; usually settled by paying QR 12,000 instead of prosecution [L0120, L0117]; no exit until paid or guaranteed [L0117]; unpaid = deportation [L0118]; minister may waive/reduce on humanitarian grounds [L0119]. "Free visa" is illegal, and a free-visa worker has no legal help if unpaid [L0178].
- **Deportation:** held up to 30 days (renewable) [L0113]; deportation ticket paid by the employer, or by the illegal employer, or the worker [L0109]. Disciplinary dismissal not appealed: no return for work for 4 years [L0112]. Leaving without the notice rules: no work permit for 1 year [L0005, L0009].
- **Drugs:** trafficking import/export death or life + QR 300-500k [L0125]; trafficking possession/transport 10-20 years + QR 200-400k [L0126]; possession or transport even without intent to trade or use 3-7 years + QR 100-200k [L0127] (this is the parcel risk: "carrying for someone" is still transport); personal use 6 months-3 years + QR 10-20k [L0128].
- **Alcohol:** drinking in public or drunk in the street up to 6 months / QR 3,000 [L0129]; importing or making alcohol up to 3 years + QR 10,000 [L0130]; dealing up to 3 years [L0131]. Drunk/drugged at work = dismissal without gratuity [L0042].
- **Absence:** more than 7 days in a row / 15 a year without reason = dismissal without notice or gratuity [L0041]; employer must report a worker who leaves within 14 days [L0108].
- **Need to know:** an official MOI warning against carrying parcels for others (searched MOI portal security awareness; customs is part S/other). Law basis exists [L0127].

### 5. If you aren't paid

- **Compulsory first step (Law 9/2026):** submit the dispute to MOL's department for amicable settlement [L0052]; settlement attempt within 7 days, result within 7 more [L0053]; if it fails, referral to the Labour Dispute Settlement Committee within 3 working days [L0054]; hearing within 7 working days, 3 days' notice [L0055]; filing stops the limitation clock [L0056].
- **Committee:** judge-chaired [L0062]; decides within 3 weeks of first hearing [L0058, L0215]; decisions immediately enforceable [L0059, L0057]; appeal to the Court of Appeal within 15 days, no automatic stay, court decides within 30 days [L0060]; notices by registered letter or any provable means [L0061]; appear in person or by any representative [L0063]; non-Arabic documents need certified Arabic translation [L0064]; case struck out if you never attend [L0065].
- **Your rights if wages are not paid:** quit without notice and keep gratuity [L0010, L0011]; since 2026, ask MOL to change employer or end the contract, with the ticket home paid by the employer [L0067]; employer faces up to 1 year prison / QR 2,000-10,000 [L0068]; ministry suspensions cannot stop your RP renewal or contract attestation [L0069]; WPS sanctions [L0089].
- **WSIF:** pays dues decided by committees then recovers from the employer [L0090, L0212]; caps 3 months' gross up to QR 20,000 (operating company), 2 months up to QR 12,000 (dissolved), 3 months up to QR 8,000 (domestic) [L0092]; application on the Fund's form with certified committee decision bearing the enforcement formula; agents need notarised POA; heirs can apply [L0093]; electronic platform [L0094]; only if not appealed within 15 days and no settlement [L0095]; **a written settlement, even unpaid, shelves the claim** [L0096]; faster if you are leaving for good [L0097].
- **Channels (official):** 24/7 hotline and 11 complaint kiosks in 11 languages at MOL branches [L0213]; MOI complaint against sponsor for abuse at the Search and Follow-up Department, free [L0144, L0145]; NHRC (Law 38/2002) [L0216]. **Channels (unofficial, not verified):** MOL Complaints and Whistleblowers portal (QID/visa no. + mobile or NAS, OTP) [L0198, L0194]; SMS "5" + QID to 92727 [L0194]; hotline 16008 [L0195]; kiosks in Bengali [L0196]; labour offices Industrial Area, Al-Shahaniya: bring passport/QID copies, company name/location, manager's contact; inspector may ask for payslips, contract, bank statements [L0197]; status by establishment no. + complaint no. + year [L0199]; NHRC office Fereej Abdulaziz [L0200]. Embassy FAQ (2021): Labour Relations Department, Sanaiya Street 13 or Al Huda Tower [L0170, L0168].
- **Cost:** **Need to know:** MOL complaint fee (none mentioned anywhere; GCO 2016: exit-grievance process free [L0219]).
- **What happens in practice (unofficial):** Q1 2026: 5,369 complaints, 2,797 settled; committees 1,190 cases, 2,228 decisions; 651 WPS violations [L0201]. Workers complain after months; resolution takes months; only ~25% reach a committee; WSIF pays only committee cases; many leave without resolution [L0221-L0223]. 2026 case: 8 months unpaid, police told workers to keep working then go home [L0202]; HRW: WPS and WSIF not effective [L0203]; embassy advised POA or change of employer [L0204]; HRW 2023: court wins not paid [L0206].
- **Bangladesh Embassy labour wing:** Counsellor (Labour) +974 4037 4725; First Secretary (Labour) +974 4467 1499, labour.doha@mofa.gov.bd [L0158, L0159]; embassy +974 4467 1927, hotline +974 3366 2000 [L0161]; advice and help in disputes [L0162]. From Bangladesh: Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare application (part B, B0024).

### 6. Changing employer, exit, absconding

- **Law:** worker may move under MOL rules (Decree-Law 19/2020) [L0122]; NOC abolished [L0210]. Notice after probation: 1 month (years 1-2), 2 months (after year 2) [L0006, L0007]; pay basic wage in lieu if short [L0008]; during probation 1 month, new employer compensates old up to 2 months' basic [L0004]. Employer can waive/shorten notice on the platform [L0191, lead].
- **E-service (lead only, MOL pages blocked):** eligibility checks [L0185]; Individuals Portal via NAS [L0187]; My Services > Change of employer within the labour market > Apply [L0189]; worker gets SMS, 3 days to approve or it is auto-rejected [L0190]; QID + own mobile [L0188]; contract authenticated, notice runs, MOI updates RP [L0186]. MOI's old page still asks for NOC/2 years [L0146, conflict].
- **In practice:** HRW 2023: employers still demand signed resignation-acceptance letters [L0205].
- **Exit:** workers may leave temporarily or finally without permission; employer can pre-list up to 5% [L0102, L0209]; Exit Grievances Committee decides in 3 working days [L0103, L0104], free [L0219]. Leave notification on Metrash is free [L0142].
- **Absconding:** the employer files the report (free; Search and Follow-up, service centres, Metrash2) [L0143] and must report within 14 days [L0108]. Government: employer cannot file after the worker has complained to MOL or a labour court; contact MOL/court quickly [L0217]. 2020 official: an earlier absconding case blocks a job change; employer must complain to MOL [L0224, L0225, lead]. Contest (Embassy FAQ 2021): go at once to the Niyabah (prosecution) court in the CID building, form "grievance against absconding report", attach ID, timesheet, agreement, attend hearing [L0173]; delay >2 months or real absconding: deportation, QR 12,000 or a ban up to 5 years; under 1 month no fine [L0174].

### 7. Leaving at the end of the job, leave, family

- **End of job:** pay all dues by next working day [L0020]; gratuity [L0013]; untaken leave cash [L0036]; service certificate and documents back [L0012]; ticket home at employer cost, procedures within 2 weeks; new employer takes over if you change [L0016, L0017]; RP cancellation (sponsor + worker sign; QR 20/50) [L0140]; leave within 90 days [L0111].
- **Going home on leave:** leave notification (free, Metrash/e-services) [L0142]; don't stay out >6 months [L0105]. Re-entry: valid RP. **Need to know:** who pays the leave ticket (law silent; contract).
- **Family:** see section 3 [L0124] and the other part's MOI family rules.

### 8. Going home without valid papers

- **Travel Permit (outpass)** from the embassy if no valid passport: form, old passport copy, 3 photos, in person interview; QR 50 / QR 98 urgent; 8:30-4 Sun-Thu; valid 3 months [L0154-L0157]. Lost passport: police GD (QR 10) first [L0184, L0177].
- **Surrender:** Search and Follow-up Department "Musallami Anfusahum" surrender section with a valid passport [L0176]; settlement QR 12,000 or deportation [L0120, L0118]; minister can waive [L0119].
- **Grace periods:** last found: 9 Feb-9 May 2025, airport or Search and Follow-up Department (Salwa Road) 1-9 pm [L0192, L0193, lead]. **Need to know:** any 2026 grace period (MOI pages/news searched).
- **Bans:** disciplinary dismissal 4 years [L0112]; notice breach 1-year work-permit bar [L0009]; a deportee by court judgment may return only by the Minister's decision [L0228]. Embassy FAQ: up to 5 years if QR 12,000 unpaid [L0174].

### 9. Death, injury, loss of job

- **Qatari side:** employer reports [L0045]; death compensation (diya) and dues deposited with the court within 15 days, distributed under the worker's country's personal status law [L0015, L0050, L0047]; employer/recruiter pays body transport if family asks, or burial in Qatar [L0018, L0110]; claim within 1 year [L0049].
- **Embassy:** body NOC; employer pays [L0163]; FAQ paperwork (Hamad Hospital death notification, certificate, Human Services Office NOC, company papers, final settlement, passport, QID copy, heirs' NIDs, receiver's ID) [L0179]; police 999 for deaths outside hospital [L0180]; local burial NOC [L0181]; compensation via POA attested in Bangladesh and Qatar, embassy lawyer, money via WEWB [L0164, L0165, L0182]; hospital visits and repatriation of poor sick [L0166]. Bangladesh-side benefits (Tk 35,000, Tk 3 lakh, insurance Tk 10 lakh) are in part B (B0056-B0077, B0140-B0152); don't duplicate.
- **Job loss:** WSIF caps for dissolved companies [L0092]; transfer rights [L0067]; Bangladesh insurance Tk 50,000 if back within 6 months (part B).
- **WEWB membership from Qatar:** QR 140 at the embassy (Nov 2025 page) [L0167] vs QR 170 in the 2021 FAQ [L0183] (conflict). Part B notes the fee rose in Bangladesh on 1 Oct 2026; check B's conflict note.

---

### Draft steps (to slot into the work-visa section)

**After arrival.** (1) Within 30 days the company takes you for medical and fingerprints; you may not refuse [L0101] (part W). (2) Ask the PRO for your QID; if none, see SOP L5. (3) Ask for your passport back once the QID is done; you may sign a written request to let the company keep it, and it must return it on request [L0098]. (4) The company opens your salary account (QID, passport, mobile) [L0207, unofficial]; wages arrive by the 7th [L0088]. (5) Get a SIM in your name (needed for Metrash and MOL services) [L0185, lead]. (6) Get the PHCC health card (QR 100) [L0149]. (7) Install Metrash (SOP L1).

**Your rights at work:** bullets from section 2.

**If you aren't paid:** SOP L3 then SOP L4; tell the embassy labour wing; from Bangladesh, family uses the Ministry application (part B).

**If you want to change employer:** SOP L6. Don't stop working before approval; an absconding report filed first can block you [L0224, lead].

**If you're reported absent (absconding):** SOP L7.

**Leaving at the end:** collect dues next working day [L0020], gratuity [L0013], certificate [L0012], ticket [L0016]; RP cancelled (both sign) [L0140]; leave within 90 days [L0111].

**Going home on leave:** leave notification [L0142]; back within 6 months [L0105].

### Draft SOPs

**SOP L1: Get Metrash and check your QID and permits.**
- When: once you have a QID.
- Steps: install "METRASH" by Ministry of Interior - Qatar (App Store id6470879050; Google Play same publisher) [L0147]; register with QID and a mobile number in your own name (**Need to know:** official steps; unofficial guides say QID + passcode + SMS). Choose English or Hindi/Urdu (no Bangla) [L0148]. Use it for leave notification (free) [L0142], RP renewal status [L0139], lost QID replacement (with PRO) [L0171].
- What you see: e-wallet with your QID and documents [L0148].
- If it goes wrong: MOI service centre with passport [L0171]; MOI 2367111 (moi-svc pages footer; not logged).

**SOP L2: Check your contract and wage rules.**
- Contract printing portal (part W, W0070): QID or visa number + mobile + SMS code, free. Compare wage with QR 1,000 + 500 + 300 [L0074]; hours [L0024], overtime [L0028], leave [L0034].
- If it differs: complaint (SOP L3).

**SOP L3: File a wage or contract complaint with the Ministry of Labour.**
- When: wages late more than 7 days after the 1st [L0088], or any breach.
- Steps (official): MOL hotline (24/7) or a complaint kiosk at an MOL branch, 11 languages [L0213]; the department must try to settle within 7 days [L0053]. Unofficial details: online portal (QID/visa no. + mobile or NAS, OTP; fill employer details, violation, upload payslips/contract/bank statements) [L0198, L0194]; SMS "5"+QID to 92727 [L0194]; hotline 16008 [L0195]; labour office with passport/QID copies, company name/location, manager's phone [L0197].
- Cost: none found (**Need to know**).
- What you get: complaint number; status by establishment no. + complaint no. + year [L0199, lead]; agreed settlement is signed and enforceable [L0053].
- If it goes wrong: after 14 days unresolved, the department must refer to the committee (SOP L4) [L0054]; ask for referral; embassy labour wing [L0158]; NHRC [L0200, lead]. Keep working or ask to change employer/end contract with ticket paid [L0067]. Don't sign a settlement unless paid: a written settlement blocks WSIF [L0096].

**SOP L4: Labour Dispute Settlement Committee and the Workers' Fund.**
- Steps: hearing within 7 working days of referral [L0055]; attend or send any representative with POA [L0063]; bring Arabic translations of documents [L0064]; decision within 3 weeks [L0058]; enforceable at once [L0059]. Appeal within 15 days [L0060].
- WSIF: if the employer doesn't pay, apply on the Fund's form/platform with the certified decision bearing the enforcement formula, after the 15-day appeal window [L0093-L0095]; caps [L0092]; say if you are leaving for good [L0097]. **Need to know:** WSIF platform URL, office, processing time (wsif.gov.qa blocked).
- If you must leave Qatar: give a notarised POA to a representative (embassy or lawyer) [L0093, L0204 unofficial].

**SOP L5: No QID, passport withheld, or abuse.**
- No QID after 30 days: ask PRO/manager/HR; then Public Prosecution residency affairs (CID building) with visa and passport [L0169] (part W too).
- Passport withheld: ask in writing; complain to MOL [L0213] / MOI Search and Follow-up (abuse complaint: passport/ID copy + written complaint, free) [L0144, L0145]; fine QR 25,000 [L0099].
- Abuse: police 999 [L0175].

**SOP L6: Change employer online.**
- Check first: no ban on you or the company, new company's registration active, no other request pending, mobile in your name, no non-compete [L0185, lead]; check no absconding report (Metrash) [L0224, lead].
- Steps (lead): new employer or you submit on the MOL platform (Individuals Portal, NAS login) [L0187, L0188]; My Services > Change of employer within the labour market > Apply > choose employer types > enter new contract > submit [L0189]; you get an SMS and must approve within 3 days in My Requests > Take Action, else it is rejected [L0190]; current employer may waive or cut notice [L0191]; Ministry decides; contract authenticated; notice runs (1 or 2 months [L0006, L0007]); MOI updates RP [L0186]. MOL fee: none reported; MOI fee unknown (old MOI page QR 1,000-3,000 [L0146] is superseded).
- If unpaid wages: request under Art. 146 bis [L0067].
- If refused: complaint (SOP L3). **Need to know:** official MOL guide (blocked).

**SOP L7: If your employer reports you as absconding.**
- Prevent: complain to MOL first if there is a dispute; an employer cannot file after you have complained [L0217].
- Check: Metrash (**Need to know:** exact menu).
- Contest (Embassy FAQ 2021): go at once to the Niyabah court in the CID building, ask for the grievance form against the absconding report, type it, attach QID, timesheet, contract; attend the hearing [L0173]. Act within 1 month [L0174]. Embassy labour wing [L0158].
- If it stands: settlement QR 12,000 or deportation and ban [L0120, L0174].

**SOP L8: Leave Qatar at the end of the job.**
- Ask for final settlement (wages, gratuity, leave pay) by the next working day [L0020, L0013, L0036], service certificate [L0012], ticket [L0016]. Both sign RP cancellation [L0140]. If not paid: SOP L3 before leaving; WSIF can pay faster if leaving for good [L0097]. Exit is free of employer permission [L0102]; if stopped, Exit Grievances Committee (3 working days) [L0103].

**SOP L9: Go home without a valid passport (Travel Permit).**
- Lost passport: police GD (QR 10; letter from employer or embassy) [L0184]. At the embassy: form, old passport copy, 3 photos, interview; QR 50 / QR 98; 8:30-4 Sun-Thu; valid 3 months [L0154-L0157]. Then clear status with MOI (SOP L10).

**SOP L10: Undocumented: surrender and leave.**
- Search and Follow-up Department (Salwa Road), surrender section, with valid passport or TP [L0176, L0192 lead]; pay settlement (QR 12,000 for absconding/overstay offences) or ask for humanitarian waiver [L0120, L0119]; watch for MOI grace periods [L0192].

**SOP L11: A worker dies in Qatar.** See part B SOP; Qatar steps: police 999 if outside hospital [L0180]; Hamad documents [L0179]; embassy NOC [L0163]; dues and compensation deposited with court in 15 days [L0015, L0050]; POA for compensation [L0182, L0164]; claim within 1 year [L0049].

### Need to know (with what was searched)

1. Official MOL complaint channels, hotline number and fees; portal URL (mol.gov.qa blocked; Wayback shells; Firecrawl tunnel error; Hukoomi slug unknown; GCO gives "24/7 hotline", no number).
2. Official MOL change-employer guide; MOI fee for RP transfer after 2020 (MOI page outdated).
3. Official Metrash registration steps and where an absconding report shows (MOI portal, App Store).
4. Who pays RP/QID fees; whether workers can be charged (Labour Law, MOI pages).
5. Whether compulsory health insurance applies to resident workers now (Al Meezan decisions list, GCO).
6. WSIF platform URL, office, processing time (wsif.gov.qa unreachable).
7. Any 2026 MOI grace period (news search; latest 2025).
8. Current procedure to contest an absconding report (only Embassy FAQ 2021 and 2020 news).
9. SIM ID rules (Ooredoo/Vodafone official pages not fetched).
10. QCB rule on worker bank accounts.
11. Who pays the ticket for annual leave (Labour Law silent).
12. Official MOI warning about carrying parcels.

### Conflicts

- **Change of employer:** Decree-Law 19/2020 [L0122] and GCO [L0210] (no NOC) vs old MOI page (NOC, 2 years, fees) [L0146]. Law is the rule.
- **WEWB membership fee in Qatar:** QR 140 (Embassy page Nov 2025) [L0167] vs QR 170 (Embassy FAQ 2021) [L0183]; part B also has this.
- **Notice periods in guides** ("one month during probation", "30/60 days") match the law [L0004, L0006, L0007]; older guides citing 2017 rules are outdated.
- **Gratuity:** law says at least three weeks per year [L0013]; some guides say 21 days (equal) or more.

### Blocked

See `blocked-parts/L.md`: MOL services, complaints, change-employer guide, complaint platform; WSIF site; NHRC; Hukoomi labour services; MOI Metrash registration; 2026 grace period; MOPH insurance for residents; Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare Facebook posts on Qatar.


## B: B

## Findings, part B: Bangladesh-side steps, money home, coming home, Qatar corridor numbers

Researched 7 Nov 2026. Ledger ids B0001-B0173 in `ledger-parts/B.csv` (facts in `B.facts.csv`): 87 verified, 65 possibly-outdated, 12 conflict, 4 lead, 5 unofficial.

**How the Malaysia work was reused.** B0001-B0114 are the generic Bangladesh-side rows from Malaysia part B (and R0012), re-added through `ledger.py add` against raw files copied from `countries/malaysia/raw/` and logged with their original URLs and methods (so every text-layer quote was re-checked by the tool). Rows Malaysia transcribed from page images keep `--no-quote-check` and the original transcription note; I re-rendered and re-read the welfare-fee circular (B0055: Tk 3,500 to Tk 5,500 from 1 Oct 2026, memo 49.00.0000.024.02.007.26.1078, 20 Sep 2026) and confirmed it. Each reused row's note says "Reused from Malaysia Bxxxx"; ids inside conflict notes were remapped to the Qatar ids. Access dates on reused rows show 7 Nov 2026 (the day they were logged here); the pages were fetched by the Malaysia researcher on 6 Nov 2026.

**Newer versions re-checked.** The Ministry's notices list (7 Nov 2026, B0162) and the Welfare Board's notices list (`raw/wewb-notices-2026-11`) show no welfare-fee or insurance circular newer than 20 Sep 2026; the newest Ministry items are BOESL recruitment notices (Malaysia, Jordan, Morocco, Singapore) and the 22 Sep 2026 complaint-cell notice (B0031). The Embassy in Doha's membership page (Nov 2025) still shows QR 140, set before the fee rise (see Conflicts).

**Caution on two Bangladesh Bank PDFs.** The pdftotext layers of `bb-top30-country-remittance` and `bb-remittance-cost` shift rows and columns (the text layer puts USA's 2.08/1.44 against Qatar and Greece's figures against Qatar in the top-30 table). I rendered both PDFs and transcribed the Qatar rows from the images (B0118-B0120). The Malaysia row B0093 ("Malaysia USD 666.73m, 5th") matches the image; anyone reading the .txt directly will get wrong numbers.

### Notes by doc section

#### Step: register on OEP and train (all work routes to Qatar)
- OEP (Overseas Employment Platform, BMET's online system): open employee.oep.gov.bd/employee/login-with-otp in Chrome, enter details exactly as on the NID (drop dots, correct spaces), verify by OTP, fill the full form, pay by bKash/Nagad, download the Registration Card [B0001]. The fee amount is not stated anywhere official [B0001]. OEP hotline 09614445271 [B0003]. OEP is also where you apply for TTC training, the 3-day PDO and clearance [B0004].
- TTC course (Technical Training Centre) through OEP: Training portal, Trade/Skills, division/district/centre, Search, Apply, Process to Enroll, Confirm [B0002]; 55 trades; short-course fee set by BMET against a money receipt; project-funded courses free [B0005].
- If anyone offers to fix TTC admission/passing or clearance for money, tell the police [B0007]; BMET asks people not to use middlemen and to pay only through proper channels against a receipt [B0019].

#### Before paying: checks, payment, complaints
- Pay the agency only into its bank account and keep receipts [B0009]. Checklist of papers before going [B0008]; copies at home and with you, two bank accounts [B0010]; never carry others' packets [B0012].
- **Visa check at BMET: Qatar visas are checked online** (with Saudi Arabia, Bahrain, Singapore, UAE), plus a visa-check app on Google Play; free, 3 working days; bring the visa and the passport page with the visa stamp [B0014, B0015]. Or check at BMET Dhaka / district manpower office [B0011]. (Part A should save the online checker URL and what a genuine result shows.)
- **"Free visa" scam:** the Ministry said in Oct 2025 that no "free visa" category exists for work in Qatar [B0165 lead, BSS news; ministry original blocked].
- Complaint to BMET: application with proof to the DG, BMET; inquiry and hearing; free; 30 working days; BMET recommends, the Ministry decides [B0016, B0017]. GRS ladder via Director (Employment), then Secretary, then grs.gov.bd [B0018].
- Complaint to the Ministry (enforcement branch): application + proof + your full address + agency RL number to sasenforcement@probashi.gov.bd; starts within 3-5 working days, settled in 30 [B0020, B0021]. Agencies that sent workers without permission: application to the Secretary, 60 working days [B0022]. Mobile courts under the 2013 Act [B0023]. Ministry GRS [B0029].
- **New: Ministry Complaint Resolution Cell (22 Sep 2026):** Bangladeshis abroad can email problems/complaints to minister.expat@gmail.com; presented daily/weekly to the Minister and Secretary [B0031].
- From Qatar: unpaid wages, allowances or insurance: application to the Ministry's Welfare-1 branch [B0024]; harassment of women workers [B0025]; help to bring a worker home [B0028].

#### PKB migration loan (Probashi Kallyan Bank)
- Up to Tk 3 lakh, 9% simple, up to 3 years incl. grace, monthly instalments [B0036]; eligibility [B0032]; documents incl. visa, BMET card, 3 MICR cheques, PKB savings account [B0033]; re-entry visa extras [B0034]; 2 guarantors [B0035]; fees 0.5% + 0.5% + Tk 900/lakh risk cover, stamps; helpline 16238, +880 9677-787878 [B0037]; online on mygov.bd, pay after decision [B0038, B0039]; women's loan same terms [B0040]; loans dept 01716-175560, migration@pkb.gov.bd [B0041]; GRS gm2@pkb.gov.bd [B0042].
- PKB loan page undated (possibly-outdated); charter Sep 2026.

#### Police clearance
- Online at pcc.police.gov.bd: register, Apply, addresses (must be in the passport and where you live), upload the attested passport page, pay [B0043, B0044]; status by SMS "PCC S <ref>" to 26969 [B0045]; someone else can collect with an attested authorisation [B0046]; DMP one-stop numbers [B0048].
- **Fee conflict:** portal Tk 1,500 challan code 1-7301-0001-2681 or card [B0043] vs police.gov.bd Tk 500, code 1-2201-0001-2681 [B0047] and the Embassy Doha PCC page (2021) Tk 500, code "1-2201-001-2681" [B0150].
- **From Qatar:** PCC comes from the police station of the permanent address in Bangladesh; apply online yourself or through a nominated relative; attest the passport photocopy first [B0150]; the passport scan can be attested by the embassy if abroad [B0044].

#### Attestation / apostille
- Bangladesh joined the Apostille Convention (in force 30 Mar 2025) [B0127]. **Qatar is not a party** (absent from the HCCH table, last update 30 Jun 2026) [B0128], so Bangladeshi documents for Qatar go through attestation.
- Embassy Doha: documents from Bangladesh must first be attested by MOFA Dhaka; Embassy fees QR 37 regular / QR 55 express (first 5 pages, education and other), QR 8 per extra page; commercial QR 110 / QR 201 [B0149]. FAQ (2021): SSC/HSC via board + Education Ministry + MOFA; degrees verified by the university then Education Ministry + MOFA; birth/nikah/marriage via Law Ministry + MOFA; **PCC via MOFA** [B0148].
- **Need to know:** whether Qatar (MOFA Qatar / Qatar Embassy Dhaka) requires the PCC and certificates to be attested by the Qatar Embassy in Dhaka before travel, and the fee (Part A/D; Qatar Embassy Dhaka site blocked).

#### Pre-departure orientation and BMET clearance
- Compulsory 3-day PDO at 70 institutes, fee set by the institute against a receipt [B0006].
- Agency clearance: 3 working days; documents incl. attested OEP card, Tk 300 stamp undertaking, pay-orders, tax challan [B0049, B0050]. Own visa: apply yourself at BMET emigration branch (Dhaka) or DEMO Chattogram [B0052]; smart card fee Tk 250 [B0053]. Director (Emigration) +8802222222415, 01711-135901, diremi@bmet.gov.bd [B0054].
- **Amounts now:** welfare fee **Tk 5,500 from 1 Oct 2026** [B0055] (BMET charter still says Tk 3,500 [B0051]); insurance Tk 1,000 one-time [B0051, B0056]; clearance fee Tk 250 per worker; source income tax Tk 400 (unattested visa) / Tk 250 (attested) single visa, Tk 1,200 skilled / Tk 800 unskilled group visa [B0051]. My arithmetic (not in any source): Tk 5,500 + 1,000 + 250 + tax.
- Insurance: Tk 10 lakh death or total disability, 10-90% partial, Tk 50,000 if the job is lost and you return within 6 months; 5 years from the card [B0056-B0058]; Board helps claim [B0060].
- Airport welfare desks check smart cards; 1,098 stopped in FY2023-24 with no/fake clearance [B0061, B0064].

#### After arrival: join the Welfare Board from Qatar (if you went without BMET clearance)
- Embassy Doha (page Nov 2025): any expatriate in Qatar without a BMET clearance card can join; apply online at www.wewb.gov.bd, print the information page, submit it at the Embassy, pay **QR 140** over the counter [B0138, B0139]. FAQ (2021): bring the online application copy, passport and **Qatar ID** copy; **QR 170**; anyone under 59; BMET card holders are members automatically [B0141, B0142].
- WEWB service page (Oct 2026): online at portal.wewb.gov.bd, via the embassy labour wing, documents: application copy, passport, proof of legal work/work permit; fee **the equivalent of Tk 6,500 in local currency including life insurance** [B0065]. Check your clearance/membership at portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check [B0066]. Cards carry a QR code verifiable at member.wewb.gov.bd [B0067].
- Benefits listed by the Embassy: school quota, scholarships, help when sick, PKB loans, Tk 3 lakh to heirs [B0140].
- Wage Earner Development Bond can be bought in Qatar at Arabian Exchange Co main branch, Mushaireb (2021) [B0155].

#### Sending money home from Qatar
- Use legal channels, not hundi [B0013].
- **Cost (official):** Bangladesh Bank: average cost of sending up to USD 200 from Qatar 4.55% (Jan-Mar 2026) and **5.11% (Apr-Jun 2026)**, higher than the all-country 2.94%; overall average from Qatar 1.28% [B0119, B0120]. World Bank (Aug 2025, older): QAR 730 cost 7.18% on average (QAR 20 fee + 4.44% margin); Eastern Exchange 6.72%, Ezremit 6.77%, Al Dar Exchange 7.52%, Western Union 7.70%; QAR 1,820 cost 5.54% [B0122, B0123].
- **bKash from Qatar:** partners Gulf Exchange, Doha Exchange, Commercial Bank of Qatar, Western Union, MoneyGram [B0124]; give the receiver's bKash number and full registered name; up to Tk 2.5 lakh per transfer; ATM cash-out Tk 7 per thousand [B0125]; 2.5% government incentive (and sometimes 2.5% bank incentive) [B0126]. Gulf Exchange-bKash partnership (Sep 2025, news) [B0171].
- **Licensed check:** QCB's Other Financial Institutions Supervision Department supervises exchange houses [B0160]; QCB consumer protection 44456412 / 44456456 [B0161]. **Need to know:** QCB's public list of licensed exchange houses (not found; blocked).
- 2.5% incentive: bKash [B0126] and TBS June 2026 [B0113 lead]; Bangladesh Bank circular not found.

#### If a worker dies, is injured or loses the job (Qatar specifics)
- Body home: employer pays all costs and needs an Embassy NOC; exceptions handled with relatives, community or WEWB [B0152]. FAQ (2021): papers from Hamad Hospital (notification of death, death certificate, repatriation NOC from the Human Services Office), company papers, final settlement, original passport, QID copy, heirs' NIDs and the airport receiver's NID/passport and phone; outside hospital or accident: call police on 999 and get a police report; submit to the Embassy labour wing for an NOC; take the NOC to the Hamad morgue [B0143, B0144]. Local burial: Public Health Department NOC, heirs' certificate and heirs' no-objection [B0145].
- Death compensation (workplace/road accident): heirs give the Embassy a power of attorney (attested by Law Ministry and MOFA in Bangladesh; then Qatar MOFA; copy to Public Prosecution); Embassy lawyer; cheque to the Embassy, then WEWB, then heirs [B0146, B0151]; labour wings recovered Tk 127.6 crore in 1,852 cases FY2023-24 [B0093].
- Bangladesh side (generic): Tk 35,000 airport cheque [B0076, B0077, B0062]; Tk 3 lakh grant (clearance or membership; death in the country of clearance) [B0070-B0075]; insurance [B0056, B0057]; Ministry help for bodies incl. undocumented workers [B0027]; Board body/burial applications [B0084, B0085]; compensation recovery [B0086].
- Sick/injured: Embassy visits hospitals and helps poor and sick workers go home on request [B0154]; Board helps bring home [B0083]; treatment aid up to Tk 2 lakh with clearance/membership [B0081, B0082]; free airport ambulance [B0080].
- No Welfare Board safe home in Qatar [B0089, B0090].
- Labour disputes: labour court or the online platform under Qatari law; Embassy advice [B0153] (Part C covers the Qatar side).

#### Coming home
- Customs at HSIA: duty-free 100 g gold jewellery, 200 g silver; gold bar up to 117 g once a year at Tk 5,000 per tola; 2 used + 1 new phone a year; no alcohol; FMJ form over USD 10,000 [B0098-B0102]; customs emergency +88 01319-056591 [B0103].
- Airport desk help (customs, lost luggage, complaints, food for outpass returnees, cash in war/disaster/layoff) [B0078, B0079]; Wage Earners' Centre [B0095, B0096]; Probashi Lounge [B0097].
- Reintegration: PKB rehabilitation loan [B0104-B0107]; RAISE (ended June 2026) [B0108, B0109]; children's scholarships [B0110]; family protection [B0111, B0088]; BRAC Migration Programme [B0112].

#### Qatar as a destination (corridor numbers)
- **BMET clearances to Qatar** (BMET table to May 2025) [B0115]: 2004 1,268; 2005 2,114; 2006 7,691; 2007 15,130; 2008 24,548; 2009 11,672; 2010 12,085; 2011 13,111; 2012 28,801; 2013 57,584; 2014 87,575; 2015 123,965; 2016 120,382; 2017 82,012; 2018 76,560; 2019 50,292; 2020 3,608; 2021 11,158; 2022 24,447; 2023 56,148; 2024 74,422; 2025 (Jan-May) 40,308. Total 924,881 = 6.63% of all departures 2004-May 2025 (Saudi 34.49%, UAE 15.82%, Oman 11.85%, Malaysia 9.30%) [B0116].
- **OEP live counts:** 2023 56,178 (5th) [B0129]; 2024 74,465 (3rd) [B0130]; **2025 107,598 (2nd after Saudi Arabia)** [B0117]; **2026 to 6 Nov 53,611 (3rd after Saudi Arabia and Singapore)** [B0131]. OEP has only part of 2022 [B0114].
- **Remittances:** Jul-Sep 2026 USD 362.54m (10th; July 121.59, Aug 113.09, Sep 127.85) [B0118]; FY2021-22 USD 1,346.47m, FY2022-23 USD 1,452.69m [B0121].
- **Bangladeshis in Qatar:** Embassy: "almost half a million" (Apr 2026) [B0156]; Ministry statement via BSS: 425,681 working, 111,662 went Sep 2024-Sep 2025 [B0164 lead]. Qatar does not publish residents by nationality; NPC labour force data (via GLMM) split only Qatari/non-Qatari: 2,112,589 non-Qatari of 2,239,938 employed in 2024 [B0163 lead]. **Need to know:** any official Qatari breakdown by nationality.
- **Bans/restrictions history (news only):** 2017 blockade: departures Jan-Oct 2017 down 31% [B0167]; Qatar paused visa approvals for Bangladeshis for about six months in 2019-early 2020 to balance quotas, then promised restart [B0166]; COVID 2020 collapse visible in the numbers (3,608) [B0115]; Oct 2025 Ministry denied rumours of a visa stop [B0164]. BMET's only Qatar notice is about returning workers stranded by COVID (18 Nov 2020; `raw/bmet-notices-search-qatar`, part A's fetch). No official ban by either government found.
- **Growth (official):** Qatar's Labour Ministry strategy 2024-2030: Qatarisation of private jobs 17% to 20%, attract skilled talent, high-skilled share of expatriates 20% to 24% [B0168]; Qatar a key destination since 1976; 2025 agreement on Bangladeshi personnel in the Qatar Armed Forces [B0157, B0158]. News: Qatar wanted security and hospitality workers for the World Cup (2020) [B0166]; ~85% of Bangladeshis in construction (2017) [B0167].
- **Practical (unofficial):** low-skilled job seekers to Qatar and other Gulf states pay Tk 4-10 lakh (TBS, Dec 2025) [B0169]; BBS 2020 survey average Tk 4.02 lakh for Qatar (Daily Star 2022) [B0170].

#### Contacts
- **Bangladesh Embassy Doha:** Bldg 153, St 820, Zone 43, New Al-Hilal, PO Box 2080 [B0136]; phone +974 44671927, **hotline +974 33662000**, mission.doha@mofa.gov.bd, bdootqat@gmail.com [B0135]; hours Sun-Thu 08:30-16:30, applications 08:30-13:00 and 13:30-15:30 [B0137].
- **Labour wing:** Counsellor (Labour) +974-4037 4725; First Secretary (Labour) +974-4467 1499, labour.doha@mofa.gov.bd [B0132, B0133] (2024 report also gives +974-5001-0428 mobile and counselordoha@ / fsdoha@probashi.gov.bd [B0159]). Passport & visa wing +974-4039 4628 [B0134].
- **Probash-Bondhu call centre** (WEWB) 16135 toll free, +8809610102030 from abroad, 24/7 [B0172, B0069, B0068]. Ministry complaint cell minister.expat@gmail.com [B0031].
- BMET Director (Emigration) [B0054]; OEP hotline 09614445271 [B0003]; PKB 16238 / +880 9677-787878 [B0037], 01716-175560 [B0041].
- Airport desks [B0063, B0080]; HSIA hotline 13600 [B0103]; PCC SMS 26969 [B0045].
- QCB consumer protection 44456412 / 44456456 [B0161].
- **Qatar Embassy Dhaka:** blocked (see Blocked).

### Draft steps (with the how)

**Step: register on OEP** (before talking to any agency)
1. Open employee.oep.gov.bd/employee/login-with-otp in Chrome; type your mobile number and the OTP [B0001].
2. Fill in everything exactly as on your NID (no dots in the name) [B0001].
3. Pay by bKash or Nagad (account number, OTP, PIN) and download the Registration Card [B0001]. **Need to know:** the fee.
4. Problems: OEP hotline 09614445271 [B0003].

**Step: check the visa before paying**
1. Use BMET's online visa check (Qatar is covered) or the Google Play app; or take the visa and the passport page with the visa stamp to BMET or the district manpower office; free, 3 working days [B0014, B0015, B0011].
2. Pay the agency only into its bank account; keep receipts [B0009].
3. If someone offers a "free visa" to Qatar, the Ministry says that category doesn't exist [B0165 lead].

**Step: police clearance** (SOP 3) and attestation through MOFA Dhaka [B0148, B0149]; Qatar is not an Apostille party [B0128].

**Step: PDO and BMET clearance**
1. Do the 3-day PDO; pay the institute's fee against a receipt [B0006].
2. The agency applies for clearance (3 working days) [B0049]; own-visa workers apply at BMET emigration branch or DEMO Chattogram with the listed papers [B0052].
3. Expect to pay: welfare fee Tk 5,500 (from 1 Oct 2026), insurance Tk 1,000, clearance fee Tk 250, income tax Tk 250-400 (single visa) or Tk 800-1,200 (group) [B0055, B0051].
4. Keep the smart card: the airport desk checks it [B0061].

**After arrival in Qatar:** save the Embassy hotline +974 33662000 and labour wing numbers [B0135, B0132]. If you came without BMET clearance, join the Welfare Board (SOP 4).

**Sending money home:** SOP 7.

### Draft SOPs

**SOP 1. Register on OEP**
- When: before paying any agency or applying for training or clearance.
- Steps: employee.oep.gov.bd/employee/login-with-otp (Chrome) -> mobile number -> OTP -> details exactly as on NID (no dots, correct spaces) -> Save and continue -> full form -> pay by bKash/Nagad (account number, OTP, PIN) -> download the Registration Card [B0001]. For training: Training portal -> Trade/Skills -> division, district, centre -> Search -> Apply -> Process to Enroll -> Confirm -> My Course [B0002]. Cost: **Need to know**.
- What you see: a downloadable Registration Card; the course appears under My Course [B0001, B0002].
- If it goes wrong: OEP hotline 09614445271 [B0003]; Probash-Bondhu 16135 [B0069].

**SOP 2. Get BMET clearance and the smart card**
- When: after the visa, contract and PDO, before the flight.
- Steps (agency visa): the agency submits the application, visa copy, attested contract, attested OEP card copy, Tk 300 stamp undertaking, pay-order for welfare fee and smart card fee, income-tax challan; 3 working days [B0049, B0050]. Own visa: you apply at BMET emigration branch (Dhaka) or DEMO Chattogram with your application, visa copy, contract if any, attested OEP card copy, Tk 300 non-judicial stamp declaration, pay-order for welfare and insurance fees [B0052]. Fees: welfare Tk 5,500 [B0055], insurance Tk 1,000, smart card/clearance Tk 250, income tax as above [B0051, B0053].
- What you see: a BMET smart card; it is your insurance (Tk 10 lakh, 5 years) and Welfare Board membership [B0056, B0066, B0142]; check it at portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check [B0066].
- If it goes wrong: Director (Emigration) +8802222222415, 01711-135901, diremi@bmet.gov.bd [B0054]; BMET GRS [B0018]. Without a card you can be stopped at the airport [B0061, B0064].

**SOP 3. Get police clearance (PCC)**
- When: the employer, visa process or attestation asks for it; or you are in Qatar and need one.
- Steps: pcc.police.gov.bd -> register -> Apply -> personal details -> present address (must be in your passport, where you live) -> upload the passport data page attested by a first-class gazetted officer (or by the embassy if abroad) -> pay [B0043, B0044]. Fee: the portal asks Tk 1,500 by treasury challan (code 1-7301-0001-2681) at Bangladesh Bank/Sonali Bank or by card [B0043]; police.gov.bd and the Doha Embassy (2021) still show Tk 500 (code 1-2201-0001-2681) [B0047, B0150]. From Qatar: apply online yourself or through a nominated relative; the certificate comes from the police station of your permanent address [B0150]. For use in Qatar, have it attested by MOFA Dhaka [B0148].
- What you see: track by SMS "PCC S <reference>" to 26969 [B0045]; a relative can collect it with an attested authorisation letter [B0046].
- If it goes wrong: PCC helpdesk 01320001824, 01320001825, help.pcc@police.gov.bd (Sun-Thu 9-5) [B0045]; DMP one-stop 01320037339 [B0048]. **Need to know:** processing time; the current official fee.

**SOP 4. Join the Welfare Board from Qatar** (only if you went without BMET clearance)
- When: you are in Qatar without a BMET smart card (check first at portal.wewb.gov.bd/bmet-wewbm-check [B0066]).
- Steps: apply online at portal.wewb.gov.bd (Embassy page says www.wewb.gov.bd) [B0065, B0138]; print the information page shown after applying [B0139]; take it with your passport and Qatar ID copy (and proof of legal work/work permit) to the Bangladesh Embassy, New Al-Hilal, Sun-Thu 08:30-13:00 or 13:30-15:30 [B0141, B0065, B0137]; pay at the counter. Fee: **conflict**: QR 140 (Embassy page, Nov 2025) [B0138], QR 170 (Embassy FAQ, 2021) [B0141], "Tk 6,500 equivalent in local currency incl. life insurance" (WEWB, Oct 2026) [B0065]; the welfare fee rose on 1 Oct 2026 [B0055]. Ask the Embassy for the current amount.
- What you see: membership card with a QR code verifiable at member.wewb.gov.bd [B0067]; benefits: Tk 3 lakh to heirs, scholarships, sickness aid, PKB loans [B0140].
- If it goes wrong: labour wing +974-4467 1499, labour.doha@mofa.gov.bd [B0133]; Probash-Bondhu +8809610102030 [B0069]; WEWB complaints focal person [B0094].

**SOP 5. Apply for a PKB migration loan**
- When: you need up to Tk 3 lakh for migration costs.
- Steps: at a PKB branch near your permanent address, or on mygov.bd (one application at a time; fees after the decision) [B0038, B0039]; bring the free form, 3 photos, NID, passport, visa, BMET card (not for re-entry), contract (optional), 3 signed MICR cheques + statement, PKB savings account (min Tk 500) [B0033]; 2 guarantors with photo, NID and address proof [B0035]. Terms: 9% simple, up to 3 years, monthly instalments, decided in 7 working days [B0036]. Fees: 0.5% + VAT processing, 0.5% + VAT documentation, Tk 900 per lakh risk cover, stamps [B0037].
- What you see: approval and disbursement into your PKB account [B0036].
- If it goes wrong: 16238 / +880 9677-787878 [B0037]; migration@pkb.gov.bd, 01716-175560 [B0041]; PKB GRS gm2@pkb.gov.bd [B0042].

**SOP 6. Complain about an agency**
- When: an agency took money and didn't send you, sent you to a different job, or broke the contract.
- Steps: (a) BMET: self-explanatory application with proof (receipts, contract, visa) to the DG, BMET, Kakrail; free; hearing; 30 working days [B0016, B0017]. (b) Ministry enforcement branch: application + proof + your full address + the agency's RL number to sasenforcement@probashi.gov.bd; starts in 3-5 working days, settled in 30 [B0020, B0021]. (c) From Qatar: email minister.expat@gmail.com (Ministry complaint cell) [B0031]; unpaid wages/insurance abroad to the Welfare-1 branch [B0024]; labour wing in Doha [B0132].
- What you see: a hearing date and a decision by the Ministry [B0017].
- If it goes wrong: GRS ladder (Director Employment, then Secretary, then grs.gov.bd) [B0018]; Ministry GRS [B0029]; mobile courts [B0023].

**SOP 7. Send money home through a licensed channel**
- When: every transfer from Qatar.
- Steps: use a bank or an exchange house that Bangladesh's partners list (for bKash: Gulf Exchange, Doha Exchange, Commercial Bank of Qatar, Western Union, MoneyGram) [B0124]; give the receiver's bKash number (or bank account) and full name as registered; hand over the cash [B0125]. Compare costs: sending small sums (under USD 200) from Qatar cost 5.11% on average in Apr-Jun 2026 [B0119]; the World Bank found 6.72%-7.70% for QAR 730 and 5.08%-6.06% for QAR 1,820 (Aug 2025) [B0122, B0123]: sending larger amounts less often costs less per riyal [B0120, B0122]. Keep the receipt.
- What you see: the family gets the money plus the 2.5% government incentive (sometimes 2.5% bank incentive) [B0126]; bKash cash-out at ATMs Tk 7 per thousand [B0125].
- If it goes wrong: QCB consumer protection 44456412 / 44456456 [B0161]; bKash helpline 16247, +88-02-55663001 [B0173]. Don't use hundi [B0013]. **Need to know:** QCB's list of licensed exchange houses.

- **OEP registration fee** [B0001]: OEP pages, BMET charter, TTC guide (Malaysia's search; not re-run).
- **PCC fee and processing time** [B0043, B0047, B0150]: portal, police.gov.bd, DMP, Embassy Doha.

- **Qatar Embassy Dhaka contacts**: blocked.
- **Whether Qatar requires Qatar Embassy Dhaka attestation of the PCC/certificates** (Part A/D).
- **Government-fixed maximum migration cost for Qatar**: not researched here (Part A: recruitment and costs). News mentions Tk 1,00,780 (OpenDemocracy, not fetched).

### Conflicts
- Welfare Board membership fee from Qatar: B0065 (Tk 6,500 equivalent, WEWB Oct 2026) vs B0138 (QR 140, Embassy Nov 2025) vs B0141 (QR 170, Embassy FAQ 2021). Official vs official; show all; WEWB page newest.
- PCC fee: B0043 (Tk 1,500, portal) vs B0047 and B0150 (Tk 500).
- Welfare fee: B0055 (Tk 5,500 from 1 Oct 2026, circular) vs B0051 (Tk 3,500, BMET charter Sep 2026). Circular is the rule.
- Insurance start: B0058 (1 Jan 2023) vs B0059 (12 Mar 2023). No effect for people leaving now.
- Treatment aid: B0081 (up to Tk 2 lakh, Oct 2026) vs B0091 (Tk 1.5 lakh, 2024). Newer wins.
- Ambulance: B0080 (free) vs B0092 (low-cost, 2024).
- Not real conflicts: OEP live counts (B0129 56,178; B0130 74,465) vs BMET table (B0115 56,148; 74,422): different cuts. Labour wing emails labour.doha@mofa.gov.bd (B0133, 2026) vs counselordoha@/fsdoha@probashi.gov.bd (B0159, 2024); office numbers match.

### Blocked
See `blocked-parts/B.md`: Qatar Embassy Dhaka pages (2), NPC labour force bulletin, QCB licensed exchange-house list, Bangladesh Bank FEPD circular 108 (incentive), newer BB country-wise remittance table, Ministry Facebook statement of 21 Oct 2025 and Qatar posts.

### Hand-offs to other parts
- Part A: BMET online visa check covers Qatar (B0014); "free visa" scam warning (B0165); BMET permission for BTS Overseas (RL-2434) to send women workers to Qatar (`raw/bmet-misc-search-qatar-bn`).
- Part C: Embassy FAQ (`raw/bdemb-doha-faq`, 2021) has Qatar-side procedures: unpaid wages (Labour Relations Department, LBR-Complaints@ADLSA.gov.qa), changing employer, lost passport (GD fee QR 10), passport renewal (QR 122 / QR 404).


## V: V

## Part V findings: short-stay entry to Qatar for Bangladesh passport holders (tourist/Hayya A1, A2/A3, family visit, business and work-visit, transit/stopover, events, medical, arrival, checks, visas not open, scams)

Researched 7 Nov 2026. Ledger ids V0001-V0139 (ledger-parts/V.csv), 45 sources. Status: 121 possibly-outdated, 11 conflict, 4 unofficial, 3 lead. Almost every official page is undated (Visit Qatar, Hayya, MOI), so the tool forces "possibly-outdated" even where the page was live on 7 Nov 2026; the Visit Qatar nationality checker and the Hayya FAQ are the live, current-looking sources.

Key official sources: Visit Qatar (Qatar Tourism) visa checker, read in the page code for Bangladesh (option `A1-1/bd`) [V0001-V0010]; Hayya FAQ (Firecrawl, the most detailed) [V0011-V0043]; Hayya how-to guide and interface text embedded in hayya.qa page code (transcribed) [V0044-V0053, V0132]; Hayya "Track your E-Visa" [V0054]; MOI visa inquiry page and form [V0057-V0060]; MOI "Qatar Visit Visas" and on-arrival visa pages [V0061-V0076]; MOI department service pages (footer "© 2021", several clearly old) [V0077-V0096]; archived MOI FAQ (2018 text) [V0097-V0100]; Discover Qatar / Qatar Airways transit pages [V0101-V0109]; MoPH Visitor Guide to Medical Care (PDF 5 Mar 2025) [V0110-V0113, V0133-V0135]; Law No. 21 of 2015 on Al Meezan (Arabic, consolidated, in force) [V0114-V0123].

Terms: **Hayya** = Qatar's official e-visa platform (hayya.qa and the "Hayya to Qatar" app) [V0005, V0061]. **MOI** = Ministry of Interior (immigration). **MoPH** = Ministry of Public Health. **HIA** = Hamad International Airport, Doha. **Discover Qatar** = Qatar Airways' destination management company (tours, transit visa desk). **QID** = Qatar ID card. **Metrash** = MOI's mobile app. **MVHI** = Mandatory Visitor Health Insurance.

### 1. Tourist visit: Hayya Tourist Visa (A1)

**Do Bangladeshis need a visa?** Yes. Visit Qatar's checker returns "Visa required ... Apply for an A1 Entry Visa on the Hayya portal. You will also require a hotel reservation during the application process. Once your visa is approved you will be asked to pay the visa fee." for Bangladesh [V0001]. Bangladesh is in Visit Qatar's list of nationalities that must apply before travel, QAR 100 on Hayya [V0002]. Bangladesh is not in any visa-free / visa-on-arrival group (Visit Qatar page code [V0001 note]; MOI 90-day list [V0066], 30-day list [V0067], US-only [V0068], Qatar-Oman joint [V0069], Pakistan/India/Thailand [V0070], special on-arrival Turkey/Belarus/Iran/Poland/Hungary [V0084]). MOI: tourist visas are applied for on Hayya [V0077].
- Bangladeshis living in a GCC country: A2 (GCC Resident Visa) instead [V0003]; with a valid Schengen/UK/US/Australia/NZ/Canada visa or residence: A3 (Visa with ETA) [V0004, V0035].
- Law: entry needs a valid passport and a visa stating the purpose [V0114].

**Who it's for:** anyone travelling for tourism or to visit friends and family [V0012]. No age limit; each person needs their own e-visa; children under 18 can be added as dependents [V0029].

**Requirements (Hayya):** passport valid 3+ months from arrival; proof of accommodation (hotel booking or "Hosted by Family and Friends"); return flight ticket; the Hayya e-visa [V0011, V0050, V0056].
- Hotel booking must cover the whole stay, matching flight dates, no gaps [V0015]; upload a copy with the hotel address; some applicants may be asked to book through Discover Qatar or be exempted [V0016]. Name on the booking must match the passport; only one proof, cannot be changed after upload [V0051].
- Hosted option: the host must be a Qatar resident with a valid QID and an address registered on Metrash; 1 property; max 5 guests [V0017]; hosting does not guarantee approval [V0018].
- Photo: JPG/JPEG, close likeness to passport photo, one face [V0047, V0046 note]; enter personal details exactly as in passport; re-check auto-filled fields or the application may be rejected [V0046, V0048].
- Health insurance: see section 6 (conflict).

**Steps (Hayya guide):** 1 Select Visa "Tourist Visa (A1)" > 2 Personal Information > 3 Validate Information > 4 Accommodation Confirmation > 5 Payment > 6 Hayya E-Visa [V0044]. Account = email + password after email verification [V0052]. Visit Qatar PDF (2023): register on hayya.qa or app, upload passport, upload accommodation, pay, receive approval, print PDF or use app [V0055].
- Statuses seen in the interface: Pending Submission, Pending, Pending (Ticket Verification), "Pending Accommodation Confirmation", Pending (Payment), Pending Entry Visa, Approved, Rejected [V0132].
- Accommodation must be confirmed within 30 days of the "Pending Accommodation Confirmation" email [V0014]; payment within 14 days of "Pending Payment" or the application is cancelled [V0013].
- Cannot start a second application while one is "Pending" [V0020].

**Fee:** QAR 100, paid after final approval; non-refundable whatever happens; "under certain conditions" can be exempted (no detail) [V0013, V0049, V0002].
**Processing time:** no fixed time (no SLA); apply well in advance [V0019].
**Validity / stay:** single entry [V0022, V0049]; Hayya FAQ (business section) gives tourist visas (A1, A2, A3, F1) "Validity: 30 Days, Single Entry" [V0031]; old MOI text: up to 30 days on a single trip [V0099]. Law: a visitor may not stay beyond the period unless renewed [V0117]. The e-visa shows a "Last Entry Date" [V0026]. **Need to know:** official window to enter after approval (a private guide says 90 days from issue - unofficial, not verified [V0130]).
**Flights:** book after approval; losses from travelling before issue are the applicant's own [V0021] (but a return ticket is required [V0011]).
**Result:** approved Single-Entry e-visa emailed; without it entry is refused [V0022]; download "Entry Visa", carries Entry Reference Number / Hayya Number [V0053]. Printed Hayya Card no longer valid [V0028].
**Changes:** edit free until "Last Entry Date" while outside Qatar; goes back to Pending [V0026]; new passport -> update application, travel with new passport and updated e-visa [V0027].
**Refusal / appeal:** reason shown on dashboard, sometimes none; decision at the authorities' discretion [V0023]. **Need to know:** no appeal route found (searched Hayya FAQ, MOI); only re-applying is implied.
**Extension:** Hayya FAQ: apply before expiry at the Directorate of Passport, HIA [V0025]; MOI's online portal also has "visa extension / fine payment" [V0060] (conflict). Private guide: buy MoPH-registered insurance then extend online on MOI (unofficial) [V0131]. **Need to know:** official extension fee and maximum for A1.
**Work:** not permitted on a Hayya e-visa, by law [V0024]; see section 8.
**Support:** Hayya 2022 (in Qatar), +974 4441 2022, info@hayya.qa [V0030].
**Alternative through Qatar Embassy Dhaka:** Hayya says a "regular visit visa issued by the Ministry of Interior" is still possible depending on profile [V0042]. **Need to know:** whether the Qatar Embassy in Dhaka issues visit visas to Bangladeshis (consular page blocked; see Blocked).

### 2. GCC Resident Visa (A2) and Visa with ETA (A3) for Bangladeshis abroad

- A2: Bangladeshis resident in Bahrain, Kuwait, Oman, Saudi Arabia or UAE [V0003]; GCC residence permit valid 3 months from application; travel from the country of residence [V0034]; passport + GCC ID valid 3 months from arrival, return ticket, QAR 100; stay up to 30 days from entry, single entry [V0032]. Job title must match the residence permit; "others" allowed [V0033]. Hayya FAQ also says on-arrival visas are still available to GCC residents [V0136].
- MOI on-arrival visa for GCC residents: QR 100, 30 days, single trip, extendable [V0074]; profession must be one of 206 approved professions [V0073]; the list (Oct 2023) is professional/skilled jobs, no general labour on p.1 [V0076]. Conflict with Hayya A2 "others" [V0033].
- A3: valid US/UK/Canada/Schengen/Australia/NZ visa or residency at application, return ticket, QAR 100 [V0035, V0004].
- A4 (companion of a GCC citizen): only travelling with the GCC citizen sponsor [V0036]; MOI on-arrival version is for "servants or the like" entering with the citizen [V0075].

### 3. Family visit and personal visit (MOI)

- Family visit: the host in Qatar applies through Metrash2 [V0079]; one month, extendable up to five more months for first-degree relatives; QR 200 issue + QR 200 per month extension [V0080]; host must not be in a "Labour category" profession and must earn QAR 5,000/month; visitor needs health insurance [V0081]; visitor's passport valid 6 months, proof of relationship, proof of family accommodation in the host's name [V0082]. Over one month: medical check before the end of the allowed period and apply for extension [V0095]. (Overlap with family/long-stay part.)
- Personal visit visa: only with approval of the Minister of Interior or representative; one month + one month; QAR 200/month [V0083].
- Law: the host is responsible for a visitor [V0119]; anyone housing a foreigner must report name and address to the local security department within 24 hours (fine up to QAR 20,000 for breach) [V0123].

### 4. Business visit and work visit (the Qatari company applies)

- Hayya does not handle business visas for private companies: Hayya Business serves government/semi-government bodies; the Hayya "Official Visa" (60 days) is only for government invitations [V0041].
- MOI "Businessmen Visa": the Qatar trading/investment company (with prior approval) applies through its representative or owner at MOI Services Centres; proof the visitor is a businessman, return ticket, clear passport copy; one month, not extendable; QR 200 [V0085-V0087].
- MOI lists a business visa issued in advance for 90 days [V0093]; the Minister's Decision 19/2007 text: business visit one to six months, QR 200 per month, needs an invitation letter from a Qatari establishment [V0072]. Durations conflict between MOI pages (1 month vs 90 days vs up to 6 months).
- MOI 72-hour on-arrival business visa (QR 50) is tied to Iran on the special on-arrival page; not open to Bangladeshis [V0088, V0084].
- MOI Multiple Entry Work Visit Visa: employer (government or company) brings expatriates in "rare professions"; one month, extendable to six; QR 200/month for private companies; RARC (Recruitment Authorization) approval, sponsor's establishment ID, passport copy, proof of profession/qualification; medical test if staying over 30 days [V0089-V0091].
- **Need to know:** current MOI e-service name, online route and processing time for business/work-visit visas (all MOI service pages are old; footer © 2021).

### 5. Transit and stopover

- Qatar Airways / Discover Qatar Transit Visa, QAR 100, for travellers who do not qualify for visa-free entry/visa on arrival [V0101, V0104]:
  - Transit up to 12 hours: at the Discover Qatar Transit Desk at HIA (Duty Free Plaza South, behind the escalators); immediate; needs a Discover Qatar transit tour booking and a Qatar Airways / code-share ticket [V0104, V0105, V0101].
  - Stopover 12-96 hours: online with the stopover hotel booking; 4 working days [V0104, V0105].
  - Max 96 hours, not extendable; refund less QAR 25 if refused; fee non-refundable once approved; the Discover Qatar booking becomes non-refundable once used for a visa [V0106, V0107].
  - Visas are at MOI's sole discretion; Discover Qatar refunds the tour if entry is refused despite meeting requirements [V0103].
- Visit Qatar: Qatar Airways passengers transiting 6+ hours not eligible for visa-free entry can get a transit visa at the desk for a Doha transit tour [V0007]; transit visa up to 4 days with stopover packages [V0008]. City/desert tours need 6+ hours [V0102]. Stopover packages for transits of 12-96 hours [V0108].
- Hayya Transit Visa (4 days) is available only through the aviation industry [V0040].
- Old MOI page (free transit visa 12-48 h / 4-12 h) conflicts with current QAR 100 scheme [V0092].
- MoPH: transit/temporary-entry passengers do not need MVHI [V0135].
- **Need to know:** whether Bangladeshis can transit airside at HIA without any visa (no official statement found); whether the Qatar Airways online transit-visa portal is open (blocked).

### 6. Health insurance (conflict)

- MoPH (PDF 5 Mar 2025): MVHI (emergencies and accidents) mandatory since Feb 2023 for visitors who need a prior entry visa and those extending [V0133]; QAR 50 per person per month [V0134]; only MoPH-registered/approved insurers [V0110]; covers emergency up to QAR 150,000, no co-payment [V0111]; non-emergency not covered [V0112]; helpline 16000 ext. 1, +974 4406 9963 [V0113].
- MOI: valid insurance from a MoPH-registered insurer is required to enter Qatar [V0062].
- Visit Qatar: only "recommend[s]" insurance and says it can be bought on arrival [V0006].
- Hayya FAQ points to MoPH [V0043]; Hayya interface has "Health Insurance is required" strings (transcript file).

### 7. Conference/event, medical, other

- Conference & Events Visa (Hayya "B"): for invitees of an event organiser; organiser applies; free; photo and passport valid 3 months [V0038, V0039].
- Medical: no separate medical treatment visa found on MOI's visa lists or Hayya (MOI non-work visit list: Family Visit, Official, Tourist, Personal Visit, Bilateral Agreement, Special On-Arrival, Joint Tourist, GCC companions, GCC residents). **Need to know:** visa route for a Bangladeshi coming for treatment (searched MOI, Hayya, MoPH; only a news report of MoPH "exploring" medical visas).
- Hotel/agency tourist visas (old MOI): hotels and travel agencies accredited by the tourism authority could request tourist visas, QR 100 [V0100, V0093].

### 8. Arrival at Hamad airport

- Bring: passport (3+ months), e-visa (printed or app), return ticket, accommodation proof (can be checked at any point) [V0011, V0022, V0137].
- E-gates: Visit Qatar PDF (2023) tells A1 holders to use the e-gates [V0055]; Qatar Airways says people who have entered Qatar before can use e-gates [V0109]; HIA: e-gates for eligible passengers incl. children over 130 cm [V0124]. **Need to know:** whether first-time Bangladeshi A1 visitors may use e-gates (MOI page not found).
- **Arrival card / e-registration:** none found. **Need to know** (searched Visit Qatar, Hayya, MOI; no official statement either way).
- Carrier rule: airlines must return passengers without a valid visa/passport at the carrier's cost (Law art. 5) [V0139].

### 9. Working on a visit visa, overstaying (penalties)

- Hayya: no work on a Hayya e-visa, by law [V0024]. Law 21/2015 art. 16: must not breach the purpose of entry or work for an unauthorised entity [V0118]; art. 23: employing foreigners without a contract prohibited [V0120]; art. 4: work entry visas only under an employment contract [V0115]; visa trading banned [V0116].
- Penalty (art. 38): up to 3 years' prison and/or fine up to QAR 50,000 for breaching arts. 2, 3, 4(3), 11(2) overstay, 16(1) purpose, 23(1), 24; repeat: 30 days-3 years and QAR 20,000-100,000 [V0121]. Settlement possible; cannot leave until settled or case closed unless a guarantor is given [V0122].
- Overstay fine: QR 200 per day, max QR 12,000 (MOI tips page) [V0094]; QAR 200/day (old MOI FAQ) [V0097]; MOI officer, June 2026 (news) QR 200/day [V0127].
- Visit visas cannot be converted to a work visa in Qatar; leave and re-enter (old MOI FAQ) [V0098]; Hayya CEO 2023: Hayya is not a work visa and cannot be converted (news) [V0128].
- 2026: MOI auto-extended entry visas by one month from 28 Feb 2026 (airspace disruption; overstays before that date had to pay the fine first) [V0125]; ended 7 June 2026 [V0126].

### 10. Visas and schemes NOT open to Bangladesh passport holders (access date 7 Nov 2026)

| Scheme | Bangladesh status | Official source | Ledger |

| Visa-free / free visa on arrival, 90 days (43 countries) | Not eligible (not listed) | MOI General Directorate of Passports, on-arrival visas | V0066, V0065 |
| Visa-free / free visa on arrival, 30 days extendable (39 countries) | Not eligible | MOI on-arrival visas | V0067 |
| Free 30-day on-arrival visa with Discover Qatar hotel (Pakistan, India, Thailand) | Not eligible | MOI on-arrival visas; Visit Qatar checker | V0070, V0071, V0001 |
| Visa on arrival QAR 100 (Falkland Is., French Guiana, Mauritius, Montenegro, Taiwan) / Iran / Ukraine groups | Not eligible | Visit Qatar checker page code | V0001 (note) |
| Qatar-Oman Joint Tourist Visa on arrival | Not eligible (not on MOI's 6-country or Visit Qatar's list) | MOI; Visit Qatar | V0069, V0009 |
| Special on-arrival tourist/business visas (Turkey, Belarus, Iran, Poland, Hungary); 72-hour business visa on arrival | Not eligible | MOI special on-arrival visas | V0084, V0088 |
| Visa-Free for US Citizens (F1), 2-year multiple entry | Not eligible (US only) | Hayya FAQ; MOI | V0037, V0068 |
| GCC citizens' visa-free entry | Not eligible (GCC nationals only) | Visit Qatar | V0010 |
| Hayya A2 / MOI GCC-resident on-arrival visa | Only if resident in a GCC state (on-arrival: approved professions only) | Hayya FAQ; MOI | V0003, V0034, V0073, V0076 |
