| Hayya A4 / MOI companion visa | Only travelling with the GCC-citizen sponsor | Hayya FAQ; MOI | V0036, V0075 |
| Hayya Official Visa; Hayya Business | Only via government/semi-government inviters | Hayya FAQ | V0041 |
| Converting a visit visa to a work visa inside Qatar | Not possible | MOI (archived FAQ) | V0098 |
| Work on any visit/Hayya visa | Prohibited | Hayya FAQ; Law 21/2015 | V0024, V0118, V0121 |

### 11. Scams

- **"Hayya/visit visa with a job"**: Hayya is not a work visa and cannot be converted (CEO, 2023, news) [V0128]; working on Hayya prohibited [V0024]; work entry visas only under an employment contract [V0115]. Protection: SOP V-2 (check the visa type), and the work-visa route.
- **"Free visa" for Qatar**: Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare said no such visa category exists (Dhaka Tribune report, lead) [V0129]; visa trading is a crime [V0116, V0121].
- **Fake visa websites / paying an agent for Hayya**: official channel is hayya.qa / the Hayya app [V0005, V0061]; fee is QAR 100 paid only after approval inside Hayya [V0049, V0013]; check the visa on Hayya or MOI (SOP V-2). Note: hayya.qa does not end in gov.qa. **Need to know:** an official Qatari warning against fake Hayya sites (only news on general MOI/NCSA fake-link warnings found).
- **Overstay "fixers"**: overstay fines are paid through MOI (fees2 portal "fine payment") [V0060, V0094].

### 12. Practical pass (unofficial)

- No report found of Bangladeshi visitors refused at Doha in 2025-26 (searched English and Bangla news). Searches found Bangladeshi workers stranded by the 2026 airspace closure, and the Oct 2025 "free visa" denial [V0129].
- Doha Guides (private): A1 entry permit valid 90 days from issue; stay 30 days; extension online after buying QR 50 MoPH insurance [V0130, V0131] (leads only).

### Draft steps

#### Tourist visit (Hayya A1) - steps
1. **Check you need the A1**: on visitqatar.com > Visa information, choose Bangladesh; the answer is "Visa required - Apply for an A1 Entry Visa on the Hayya portal" (A2 if you live in a GCC state; A3 if you hold a valid US/UK/Schengen/Canada/Australia/NZ visa) [V0001, V0003, V0004].
2. **Prepare**: passport valid 3+ months after arrival; a clear passport scan; a digital JPG/JPEG photo like your passport photo; a hotel booking for every night (name as in passport) or a host in Qatar with QID and Metrash address; a return ticket [V0011, V0045, V0047, V0051, V0017].
3. **Buy MVHI** from a MoPH-registered insurer (QAR 50 per month) [V0133, V0134, V0110] (conflict with Visit Qatar "recommend" [V0006]).
4. **Apply on hayya.qa or the "Hayya to Qatar" app** yourself (see SOP V-1) [V0005, V0044].
5. **Respond to statuses**: confirm accommodation within 30 days; pay QAR 100 within 14 days of "Pending Payment" [V0014, V0013].
6. **Wait** (no fixed time) [V0019]; do not travel while Pending [V0022].
7. **Download the e-visa** from the email/app; check it on Hayya "Track your E-Visa" (SOP V-2) [V0053, V0054].
8. **Fly** with passport, e-visa, return ticket, accommodation proof, insurance [V0011, V0022].
9. **At HIA**: immigration counters or e-gates if eligible [V0055, V0124].
10. **Leave before the stay ends** or extend before expiry (HIA Directorate of Passport per Hayya; MOI online menu exists) [V0025, V0060]; overstay QR 200/day up to QR 12,000 [V0094]. Do not work [V0024, V0121].

#### Transit / stopover - steps
1. Fly Qatar Airways or code-share; for under 12 hours, book a Discover Qatar transit tour and apply for the QAR 100 transit visa at the Discover Qatar desk (Duty Free Plaza South) [V0104, V0101].
2. For 12-96 hours, book a Qatar Stopover hotel package and apply online in the stopover booking (4 working days) [V0104, V0105, V0108].
3. Max 96 hours, not extendable [V0106].

#### Business visit - steps
1. Your Qatari host company (with MOI approval) applies at an MOI Services Centre for the Businessmen Visa, through its representative/owner, with your passport copy, return ticket and proof you are a businessman [V0085, V0087].
2. Fee QR 200; one month, not extendable [V0086, V0085]. For hands-on work, the employer needs a work visit visa with RARC approval (QR 200/month) [V0089-V0091].

### Draft SOPs

#### SOP V-1: Apply for a Hayya Tourist Visa (A1) yourself
- **When to use it:** you are a Bangladesh passport holder visiting Qatar for tourism or to see family/friends [V0001, V0012].
- **Steps:**
  1. Go to https://hayya.qa (or the "Hayya to Qatar" app). Sign up with your own email and a password; verify the email [V0052, V0005].
  2. Choose "Tourist Visa (A1)" [V0044].
  3. Personal Information: type details exactly as in your passport; upload the passport front page and a JPG/JPEG photo with only your face, close to your passport photo [V0046, V0047].
  4. Validate Information: re-check auto-filled fields [V0048].
  5. Accommodation Confirmation: upload one hotel booking covering all nights, in your passport name, or choose "Host Family & Friends" (host adds you on Hayya) [V0015, V0051, V0016, V0017]; deadline 30 days [V0014].
  6. Payment: QAR 100 after approval, within 14 days; non-refundable [V0013, V0049].
  7. Hayya E-Visa: download the e-visa (Entry Reference Number) [V0053].
- **What you see:** statuses Pending / Pending (Ticket Verification) / Pending Accommodation Confirmation / Pending (Payment) / Approved / Rejected [V0132]; approved = Single-Entry e-visa emailed [V0022].
- **If it goes wrong:** rejection reason on the dashboard (sometimes none); no appeal stated [V0023]; you cannot open a second application while one is Pending [V0020]; Hayya support 2022 / +974 4441 2022 / info@hayya.qa [V0030].

#### SOP V-2: Check a Qatari visit visa (or a work/visit visa) is real
- **When to use it:** someone gives you a Qatar e-visa or visa copy, or says your visa is approved.
- **Steps (Hayya e-visa):** open https://hayya.qa/en/visa-inquiry ("Track your E-Visa"); enter the Entry Reference Number, or your Passport Number and select Nationality "Bangladesh" [V0054]. Free (no fee stated).
- **Steps (MOI visas, incl. work and visit visas):** open https://fees2.moi.gov.qa/moipay/visa/inquiry?language=en (from MOI > Inquiries > Visa Services > Visa Inquiry and Printing) [V0057]; choose search by visa number (رقم السمة), passport number (رقم جواز السفر) or application number (رقم الطلب; also needs the sponsor's QID number and application date); pick the nationality; type the image code; press استعلم (Inquire) [V0059]. Issued visas can be printed or reprinted [V0057, V0058].
- **What you see:** **Need to know:** what a genuine result page shows (fields, visa type, expiry) - needs a real visa number; check that the visa type is a visit/tourist type if that is what you were promised, and remember a visit/Hayya visa never allows work [V0024, V0128].
- **If it goes wrong:** no record = do not pay or travel; Hayya support 2022 / +974 4441 2022 [V0030]; MOI 2367111 / info@moi.gov.qa [V0138]. **Need to know:** official MOI channel for reporting a fake visa.

#### SOP V-3 (draft): Buy Qatar visitor health insurance (MVHI)
- **When:** before applying for an A1 or extending [V0133].
- **Steps:** buy from a MoPH-registered insurer (list on MoPH site, blocked); QAR 50 per person per month [V0110, V0134]. MoPH helpline 16000 ext. 1 / +974 4406 9963 [V0113].
- **Need to know:** the current approved insurer list and how the policy links to Hayya (blocked MoPH pages).

### Need to know (with what was searched)
- A1 entry window after approval ("Last Entry Date"), and A1 extension fee/maximum (Hayya FAQ, MOI, Visit Qatar searched; only private guide [V0130, V0131]).
- Appeal route after an A1 refusal (Hayya FAQ, MOI searched).
- Whether the Qatar Embassy in Dhaka issues visit visas or helps with Hayya (embassy consular page blocked).
- Current MOI online route/processing time for business and work-visit visas (MOI service pages are old).
- Visa route for medical treatment (MOI, Hayya, MoPH searched; none).
- Visitor e-gate eligibility on first entry; any arrival card (HIA, MOI, Visit Qatar searched).
- Whether Bangladeshis may transit airside without a visa (Qatar Airways, Visit Qatar, MOI searched).
- Official MOI/Hayya warning on fake Hayya websites; Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare original "free visa" release (only news [V0129]).
- What a genuine MOI visa inquiry result looks like.

### Conflicts
- Health insurance: mandatory (MoPH [V0133]; MOI [V0062]) vs recommended (Visit Qatar [V0006]).
- A1 extension: HIA Directorate of Passport (Hayya [V0025]) vs MOI online extension menu [V0060] (private guide says online [V0131]).
- Passport validity: 3 months (Hayya [V0011], Visit Qatar PDF [V0056]) vs 6 months (MOI Visa Conditions [V0096]; family visit 6 months [V0082]).
- GCC residents: any job title incl. "others" (Hayya A2 [V0033]) vs 206 approved professions (MOI on-arrival [V0073]).
- Qatar-Oman joint visa list: 6 countries (MOI [V0069]) vs ~37 (Visit Qatar [V0009]); Bangladesh on neither.
- Transit visa: free 12-48 h / 4-12 h on arrival (old MOI [V0092]) vs QAR 100, up to 96 h via Discover Qatar (Qatar Airways [V0104]).
- Business visa length: 1 month not extendable (MOI Businessmen Visa [V0085]) vs 90 days (MOI visas in advance [V0093]) vs 1-6 months (Decision 19/2007 [V0072]).
- 72-hour business visa on arrival: no nationality on its page [V0088] vs Iran-only on special on-arrival page [V0084].

### Blocked
See blocked-parts/V.md: MoPH MVHI page and insurance scheme page (CAPTCHA); Qatar Embassy Dhaka consular services; Hayya logged-in screens and sample e-visa; HIA immigration/e-gates; MOI e-gates page; Qatar Airways transit-visa portal and transit-tour terms; Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare "free visa" release and visit-visa warnings (Facebook).

Note on raw files: three MOI pages were saved under misleading names during a batch fetch (moi-voa-belarus = 90-day list; moi-voa-gcc-residents = 30-day list; moi-voa-gcc-companions = US citizens page); sources.csv titles are correct. Unused misnamed raws (moi-voa-qatar-oman, moi-voa-pk-in-th, moi-voa-ukraine, moi-voa-iran, moi-voa-90days-43, moi-voa-30days-39, moi-visafree-us) are MOI department pages (intro, functions, departments, activities, forms, services list, news), not the titles their names suggest.


## S: S

## Part S findings: student visa; changing visa type; asylum; children born in Qatar

Researcher: part S. Accessed 7 Nov 2026. Ledger: `ledger-parts/S.csv` (S0001-S0136), facts: `ledger-parts/S.facts.csv`.

**Method notes.**
- Al Meezan (Qatar's legal portal, Ministry of Justice) is now a JavaScript app; curl gets an empty shell. The portal's own API (`https://api.moj.gov.qa/api/v1/Legislation/<id>`) returns the full law text as JSON. I saved the JSON with `fetch.py` and made a text copy (`raw/<name>.txt`, first line "TEXT COPY of ...", HTML tags stripped only). Laws used: Law 21/2015 (residence; another part's copy `almeezan-api-law-21-2015-residence`, English), MOI Decision 25/2019 (its executive regulations, Arabic), Law 3/2016 and Law 1/2026 (births), MOPH Decision 45/2023 (births regulations), Law 38/2005 (nationality), Cabinet Decisions 12 and 13/2019 and MOI Decision 38/2019 (asylum), Decree 18/2026 (MOI-UNHCR MoU). Law 11/2018 (asylum) came from a Wayback copy of the old Al Meezan Arabic article page (2021 capture; law shown as in force).
- Qatar University (QU) pages are JavaScript-built (SharePoint); curl gets 2-byte text. Scraped with Firecrawl and saved as verbatim excerpts. QU is a public university and the residence sponsor of its students; registered as official for its own procedures. All QU pages are undated, so `possibly-outdated`.
- The Bangladesh Embassy Doha birth-registration and e-passport notices are scanned PDFs with no text layer. Rendered with PyMuPDF at 2.2-2.5x and transcribed verbatim (Bangla) into separate `-transcription.md` files with a SOURCE line. PDF dates: 23 Jul 2023 (birth notices), 10 Jul 2024 (e-passport instructions), circular 25 Oct 2023.
- The Ministry of Labour site (mol.gov.qa) and the MOPH birth-certificate e-service did not connect; Hukoomi search fails. Ministry of Labour (MoL) facts below are from news reports (`lead`).
- Two of my early curl fetches saved empty shells and are not used: `almeezan-law-11-2018-asylum-en`, `almeezan-law-21-2015-residence-en`, `almeezan-law-21-2015-residence-api` (duplicate of another part's file), `qu-is-prospective-students`, `qu-is-rp-transfer`, `moehe-heis-list-file`, `moehe-programmes-2026-27-file` (both returned the HTML page), `bdconsularservice-home` (27 bytes).

---

### 1. Student visa (study residence permit) — feeds the "Student visa" H2

Lead paragraph idea: Qatar has no separate visa a student applies for alone; the university applies for the student's entry visa and then the residence permit (RP, the Qatar ID card) under its own sponsorship.

#### Legal basis
- The executive regulations of the residence law create a "study residence visa" for a foreign student coming to study at government schools, institutes, colleges or universities or private educational institutions; it needs a letter from the accredited institution confirming enrolment, a travel document and proof of good conduct [S0041].
- After entry, anyone coming for residence must go to the competent authorities within 30 days to finish the residence licence (medical test, fingerprints); the fine is counted from day 31 [S0042].
- A foreigner licensed for a specific purpose must not break that purpose or work for an entity not authorised; this can mean up to 3 years' prison and/or a fine of up to QR 50,000 [S0027, S0028].

#### Which universities (MOEHE list)
- The Ministry of Education and Higher Education (MOEHE) page "Higher Education in Qatar" lists the public institutions (Qatar University, Community College of Qatar, Qatar Aeronautical Academy, University of Doha for Science and Technology, military and police academies) [S0061] and 19 private ones (Hamad Bin Khalifa University, the Education City branch campuses such as Georgetown, Northwestern, VCU, Weill Cornell, Carnegie Mellon, plus Lusail University and partnership colleges such as City University Qatar with Ulster, Oryx with Liverpool John Moores, UKM Qatar, Barzan with Swinburne) [S0062].
- MOEHE also publishes a downloadable list and an alignment of all programmes for 2026-2027 [S0063]; MOEHE licenses private institutions and their programmes [S0064].
- MOEHE has an online equivalency/attestation service for degrees (eduservices.edu.gov.qa/Service.aspx?service=UCES) and may refuse degrees that do not meet its rules [S0065].

#### Qatar University: how to apply (the best-documented route)
1. Apply for admission online (QU online admission application, mybanner.qu.edu.qa); the application fee is not refunded if refused [S0016].
2. In the same semester's timeline, fill QU's online "Visa Online Application" and upload [S0001-S0008, S0012]:
   - screenshot of the admission application summary page [S0008];
   - passport copy valid at least one year [S0001];
   - Financial Sponsorship Declaration from a third party (a student cannot sponsor himself) and the sponsor's passport or Qatar ID [S0002];
   - financial proof: a sponsor living in Qatar gives a QR 20,000 bank guarantee from a Qatari bank (5 years for undergraduate/PhD, 3 for Masters, 1 for the Arabic programme); a sponsor outside Qatar gives 6 months of bank statements in English or Arabic [S0003, S0004];
   - Health Evaluation form signed and stamped by a clinic/hospital in the home country [S0005];
   - police clearance (good conduct certificate) from the home country [S0006] (Bangladesh side: Bangladesh Police online PCC, see other part);
   - parent/guardian consent letter (undergraduates only) and the Student Declaration Form [S0007, S0008].
3. QU decides through a committee; failing the visa requirements means no admission [S0009, S0012]. IELTS is optional but helps [S0017].
4. Questions: Scholarships and International Admissions Section, internationalstudents@qu.edu.qa [S0010].
- **Need to know:** QU tuition fees for international students; the MOI fee for a student residence permit; how long the student RP is issued for. Searched QU site menus (financial aid pages), web search "Qatar student residence permit fee"; only consultancy pages.

#### Scholarships
- QU International Students Scholarship (undergraduate, competitive, high-school average 95%+): no tuition or textbook fees, QR 500 a month, shared housing with transport, annual return ticket, RP under QU sponsorship; QU applies for the visa after admission [S0020-S0023].
- QU Talent Scholarship is open to international students (80%+ average; research, technology, literature, media, sport): fees waived, QR 1,000 a month, and for international students housing, ticket and QU-sponsored RP [S0024].
- **Need to know:** scholarships at HBKU, UDST and Education City campuses for Bangladeshis (not searched in depth).

#### Can a student work?
- On campus at QU: the Student Employment Program lets enrolled students (after 24 credit hours and GPA 2.5+) work in QU departments, max 5 h/day on weekdays, 7 h on weekends/holidays, 20 h/week [S0025, S0026].
- The law lets the Ministry of Labour authorise part-time work for another employer [S0031], but that article is about workers with an original job.
- Jusour (Qatar Manpower Solutions Company, chaired by the Minister of Labour) runs "Sahem", part-time jobs for students at accredited universities in Qatar [S0058]; news (2023) says users work for one employer only, must be legal residents (exchange students excluded), and there is a 1-year visa extension for recent graduates aged 18-28 on university sponsorship [S0059, S0060].
- **Need to know:** the official Ministry of Labour rule for off-campus part-time work by students on university sponsorship (hours, permit, fee). Searched web (English), QU pages, Jusour site; only consultancy guides (unofficial, not verified: 20 h/week).

#### Can a student bring family?
- No: QU says the student visa covers only the student and gives no right to sponsor family [S0011].

#### After graduation
- QU students may move their RP to a personal sponsor (father, brother, husband) or a company only after graduation [S0013]; to cancel and leave they have up to 5 days after the graduation statement; to transfer, documents within one month after final exams and transfer done within 2 months [S0014].
- A student already in Qatar on a parent's or company's RP that is being cancelled can transfer to QU sponsorship (needs GPA 2.5+) [S0018, S0019].
- Residents may stay outside Qatar up to 180 days while the RP is valid [S0015].

---

### 2. Changing visa type — feeds the "Changing visa type" section

Lead: Qatar ties each permit to a purpose and a sponsor; changing purpose needs the authority's approval, and working outside the permitted purpose is a crime.

#### The general rule
- Breaking the purpose of the permit or working for an unauthorised entity is prohibited [S0027] and punishable (up to 3 years and/or QR 50,000) [S0028].
- Work entry permits and work visas are granted only with an employment contract, certified by the authorities [S0029, S0040].
- The executive regulations let the authority, at its discretion, change the purpose of an entry visa after the holder has entered, without leaving, if the visa is still valid [S0038]; and transfer a work visa to another employer before the RP, with Ministry of Labour approval [S0039].
- **Conflict:** a 2015 law-firm alert reported Qatar stopped in-country conversion from business, arrival or tourist visas to work visas (people had to leave and re-enter) [S0131]. The 2019 regulations (official) allow a discretionary change [S0038]. Show both; the official text is the rule; whether MOI actually grants it for visit visas is a Need to know.

#### Visit visa to work
- Hayya (the visit e-visa platform) says a visitor on a Hayya e-visa is not permitted by law to work in Qatar [S0049]. The Hayya CEO said in 2023 that Hayya is not a work visa and cannot be converted into one (news) [S0050].
- **Need to know:** an MOI statement on whether any visit visa (Hayya, family visit, business) can be converted to a work RP inside Qatar today. Searched web (English, Bangla), MOI pages fetched by other parts, Al Meezan regulations. Only the discretionary Art 15(2) and the 2015 law-firm alert were found.

#### Family residence to work
- The law lets a woman's residence stay under the head of family even if she works [S0032]; if the marriage ends, a family member may transfer to another sponsor with approval [S0033].
- A husband sponsored by his expatriate wife may not work until his residence moves to the employer [S0045].
- News reports (MoL statements) describe the MoL e-service "Request to join the labour market (change from family residency to work residency)": started by the new employer or by the person entering the employer's details; needs a valid Qatar ID, active residency, National Address registration and a phone number in the person's name; after MoL approval the contract is authenticated, the employer pays fees and MOI changes the residence to work [S0051-S0055].
- **Need to know:** the MoL service page itself (fee, documents). mol.gov.qa did not connect (see Blocked).

#### Student to work
- See section 1: transfer of the QU RP to a company only after graduation [S0013, S0014]; Jusour graduate visa extension (news) [S0060].

#### Changing employer (any worker, including domestic workers)
- Since Decree-Law 19/2020, a worker may move to another employer under Ministry of Labour rules; the old Article 22 was repealed [S0030, S0048].
- Conditions in the regulations: notify the MoL, and the RP must be valid or expired less than 90 days [S0044].
- News: the MoL change-of-employer e-service covers the public, private and domestic sectors; domestic workers log in as "Labors and Individuals" with the National Authentication System [S0056]; eligibility checks (no ban, valid new employer, no pending request, phone in own name, no non-compete) [S0057].
- A domestic worker working for the employer's parents, children or spouse is not a breach [S0043].

#### Domestic worker to private-sector (company) worker
- **Need to know:** no official text found that says whether a domestic worker (under Law 15/2017) can move into a company job covered by the Labour Law, and how. The law and the e-service (news) allow a change of employer, but the switch of category is not described. Searched Al Meezan (Law 15/2017, MOI Decision 25/2019, Decree-Law 19/2020), web (English), ILO pages saved by other parts. Leads: ILO says 3,399 domestic workers' job-change applications were approved in the first nine months of 2024 (not fetched; web search result only).

#### Change of profession on the RP
- News (2020): MoL launched an online service to change the profession (job title) on the resident's ID, applied for by the person concerned or the authorised signatory with documents [S0132].
- **Need to know:** current MoL rules, documents and fee (mol.gov.qa blocked).

---

### 3. Asylum — written to discourage, facts only

Lead: Qatar has a political-asylum law, but it covers a narrow list of political cases and does not cover people who came for work or are in debt or unpaid.

- Qatar is not listed as a party to the 1951 Refugee Convention [S0090]; Human Rights Watch (2018) urged it to accede [S0091].
- Law No. 11 of 2018 on political asylum is in force [S0066]. A "political refugee" is someone outside his country who cannot return for a well-founded fear of the death penalty, corporal punishment, torture, cruel treatment or persecution because of race, religion, social group or political opinion [S0067].
- Cabinet Decision 12/2019 limits asylum to five categories: human rights defenders, journalists documenting war crimes, members of political parties, religious sects or ethnic minorities who are persecuted, writers/researchers, and former or current officials opposing their government [S0079-S0083].
- Excluded: anyone who committed serious non-political crimes abroad, war crimes, acts against UN purposes, or who has another nationality whose protection is available [S0068].
- How: applications go to the Human Rights Department of the Ministry of Interior, from the applicant, a representative, or through UNHCR [S0069, S0070]; a permanent Committee for Political Refugee Affairs examines them [S0071]; the Minister may give 3 months' renewable temporary residence [S0072]; decision within 3 months of the committee's recommendation, silence = rejection [S0073]; appeal to the Prime Minister within 30 days, final [S0074].
- After a rejection, the holder of temporary residence must leave within 60 days [S0086]. Status is cancelled if obtained with forged documents or fraud [S0077]. Refugees may not engage in political activity [S0076].
- Recognised refugees: travel document, work (not security jobs), allowance until work, health care, education, housing [S0075]; residence card valid 2 years [S0085]; non-refoulement [S0078].
- UNHCR has an office in the UN House in Lusail (opened March 2023; 9 staff in March 2026) [S0087]. In December 2025 UNHCR and MOI signed an MoU to develop national asylum procedures [S0088]; Decree 18/2026 ratified it [S0089].
- **Need to know:** whether any Bangladeshi has applied for or received asylum in Qatar, and how many people the committee has recognised. Searched UNHCR Qatar pages and factsheet, Al Meezan, web (English). None found. (The procedures were still being developed in Dec 2025 [S0088].)

---

### 4. Children born in Qatar to Bangladeshi parents

Lead: A baby born in Qatar gets a Qatari birth certificate but not Qatari nationality; parents must register the birth with the Bangladesh Embassy, get the baby a Bangladeshi passport, and then get the baby a Qatar residence permit within the deadline.

#### Qatari nationality
- Qatari nationality by origin goes to those born in Qatar or abroad to a Qatari father [S0102]; naturalisation needs 25 consecutive years of lawful residence among other conditions [S0103].

#### Step 1: Qatari birth certificate (MOPH)
- Every birth in Qatar must be reported to the Ministry of Public Health (MOPH) health offices within 15 days [S0092-S0094]; those obliged include the father, the doctor and hospital directors, and the mother [S0095].
- For a non-Qatari baby: parents' valid passports, residence permits (or entry visas for non-residents) and the marriage contract attested by official authorities (if married abroad, certified) [S0099, S0100].
- Names may not be compound [S0096].
- The parent then goes to the MOI department within 7 days to get the certificate; the first original is free [S0097, S0098].
- Unofficial, not verified: an expat guide says the application is online at eservices.moph.gov.qa/bcmoi with the hospital's Notification of Live Birth and the marriage certificate; extra copies QR 20 each [S0136].
- A child born outside marriage or of unknown father is registered only by court decision (Law 1/2026) [S0101].

#### Step 2: Bangladeshi birth registration at the Embassy
- The Embassy registers births of Bangladeshis born in Qatar [S0104]. Fee: free within 45 days of birth; QAR 10 within 5 years; QAR 10 within 10 years; correction QAR 13; re-print QAR 10 [S0105]. Counter 08:30-16:00 Sunday-Thursday [S0106].
- How (embassy notice, 2023): apply online at www.bdris.gov.bd > জন্ম নিবন্ধন > apply [S0107]; upload the MOPH birth certificate and father's passport copy, max 100 KB per file [S0108, S0109]; use a Bangladeshi mobile number and personal email [S0110]; put the Qatar address as both birthplace and present address [S0111]; print and submit at the Embassy with the original MOPH certificate and copy, both parents' passport and Qatar ID copies, and a white-background photo of the baby [S0112]; the Embassy checks and takes the fee [S0113].
- Names must have at least two words (e.g. "Sakhina Begum", not "Sakhina") (Registrar General circular) [S0117].
- Corrections: check the record at everify.bdris.gov.bd, apply on bdris.gov.bd; address proof must be notarised in Bangladesh and attested by the Law and Foreign Ministries, then by the Embassy; OTP to a Bangladeshi mobile [S0114-S0116].

#### Step 3: Bangladeshi e-passport for the baby
- The Embassy says a newborn can apply with the Bangladeshi birth registration certificate and the Qatari birth certificate [S0118]. Apply on epassport.gov.bd and book an Embassy appointment; missing it cancels the application [S0119, S0120]; enrolment in person with documents, and presence at collection [S0121].
- Under 18: online BRC (English) [S0127], father's or mother's NID number [S0128], passport 5 years / 48 pages [S0126]; under 6: 3R lab-print photo, grey background [S0129]; permanent address column must show a Bangladesh contact address [S0130].
- Fees at missions (48 pages, 5 years): workers and students USD 30 regular / USD 45 express; general applicants USD 100 / USD 150 [S0124, S0125]. The Embassy says the fee follows the profession on the Qatar ID [S0122].
- **Need to know:** which fee rate (labour/student or general) the Embassy charges for a baby's passport. Searched embassy pages and e-passport portal.

#### Step 4: Qatar residence permit for the baby
- Law: a resident licensed to have family must apply for the newborn's RP within 90 days of birth or entry [S0035]; a baby born abroad may enter within 6 months of birth [S0036, S0047]; for a baby born to a mother on a visit visa, apply within 60 days [S0037, S0046].
- **Conflict:** an expat guide (Nov 2025) says 60 days with a QR 10/day fine [S0133]; Law 21/2015 says 90 days [S0035] (official rule).
- Unofficial, not verified: apply at an MOI Service Centre or on Metrash with the original birth certificate, the baby's passport and a blue-background photo; father sponsors if the mother is on his family visa, mother if both on work visas; visa QR 200 + Qatar ID QR 500/year [S0134, S0135].
- **Need to know:** the official MOI/Metrash newborn RP procedure, deadline in practice, fine and fee. Searched Hukoomi (search broken), MOI pages fetched by other parts, Al Meezan regulations; see Blocked.

---

### 5. Practical pass leads (unofficial only)
- Bangla web search on Qatar visit-visa job scams returned Nepal cases of a non-existent "Azad visa" and visa trading; no Bangladeshi visit-to-work case found (not fetched; other parts cover scams).
- Doha Guides baby QID and birth certificate guides [S0133-S0136] describe Metrash use, fees and the 60-day practice.
- Jusour Sahem part-time jobs for students [S0058-S0060].

---

### Draft steps (with the "how")

**Student visa (Qatar University)**
1. Check the university is on MOEHE's list (SOP S1) [S0061, S0062].
2. Apply for admission online; keep the summary-page screenshot [S0008, S0016].
3. Get: passport valid 1 year+, sponsor's declaration and passport, sponsor's 6-month bank statement (English/Arabic) or QR 20,000 Qatari bank guarantee, health form stamped by a Bangladeshi clinic, Bangladesh Police clearance, parent consent (undergraduate), Student Declaration Form [S0001-S0008].
4. Upload them in QU's visa online application within the semester timeline [S0012].
5. Wait for the committee; QU applies for the visa [S0009, S0023].
6. After arrival, complete medical and fingerprints within 30 days [S0042].
7. Work only on campus (max 20 h/week) unless officially authorised [S0026, S0027].
8. At graduation: transfer the RP to an employer within 2 months or leave within 5 days [S0013, S0014].

**Baby born in Qatar**
1. Within 15 days: report the birth (hospital/MOPH) and get the free birth certificate [S0094, S0098].
2. Within 45 days: register the birth with the Bangladesh Embassy (free) via bdris.gov.bd [S0105, S0107-S0112].
3. Apply for the baby's e-passport on epassport.gov.bd and enrol at the Embassy [S0118-S0121].
4. Within 90 days of birth (law; guides say 60): apply for the baby's Qatar RP [S0035, S0133].

### Draft SOPs

**SOP S1: Check a university or programme is licensed in Qatar**
- When: before paying any agent or university for study in Qatar.
- Steps: open www.edu.gov.qa/en/Content/HigherEducationinQatar [S0061]; find the institution under "Public" or "Private Higher Education Institutions" [S0061, S0062]; download the programmes list for 2026-2027 and check the exact programme [S0063].
- What you see: the institution's name with a link to its own website. Not listed = not licensed by MOEHE as a higher education institution [S0064].
- If it goes wrong: Need to know (MOEHE complaint route for unlicensed institutions not found). To check a finished degree, use MOEHE's equivalency service eduservices.edu.gov.qa/Service.aspx?service=UCES [S0065].

**SOP S2: Apply for a Qatar University student visa** (as Draft steps above) [S0001-S0012].

**SOP S3: Register a baby born in Qatar with the Bangladesh Embassy**
- When: after the Qatari (MOPH) birth certificate is issued; free if within 45 days of birth [S0105].
- Steps: www.bdris.gov.bd > জন্ম নিবন্ধন > জন্ম নিবন্ধন এর জন্য আবেদন [S0107]; name of at least two words [S0117]; birthplace and present address in Qatar [S0111]; Bangladeshi mobile and personal email [S0110]; upload MOPH certificate and father's passport (each file 100 KB max) [S0108, S0109]; print; take to the Embassy counter (Sunday-Thursday 08:30-16:00) with original MOPH certificate and copy, both parents' passport and Qatar ID copies, white-background photo [S0106, S0112].
- Cost: free (45 days); QAR 10 later [S0105].
- What you see: the Embassy checks and processes [S0113]; check the record later at everify.bdris.gov.bd [S0114].
- If it goes wrong: correction QAR 13 via bdris.gov.bd (SOP S4) [S0105, S0114-S0116].

**SOP S4: Correct a Bangladeshi birth registration from Qatar** [S0114-S0116, S0105].

**SOP S5: Get a Bangladeshi passport for a baby born in Qatar** [S0118-S0130].

**SOP S6: Change from family residence to work** — draft only from news [S0051-S0055]; needs the MoL page (Blocked).

### Need to know (with searches done)
1. Official MOI newborn RP procedure, deadline in practice (60 vs 90 days), fine, fee — Hukoomi broken, MOI pages; see Blocked.
2. MOPH online birth certificate steps and fee — e-service blocked.
3. Off-campus part-time work rules for sponsored students — MoL site blocked; only consultancy guides.
4. Domestic worker to private-sector job — no official text found.
5. Change of profession on the RP — MoL page blocked; news only (2020).
6. Visit visa to work inside Qatar — official MOI statement not found beyond the discretionary regulation.
7. Bangladeshis and asylum in Qatar — no data.
8. QU tuition for international students; MOI student RP fee and validity.
9. Embassy passport fee rate for a baby (labour/student or general).
10. MOEHE complaint route about unlicensed institutions.

### Conflicts
- Newborn RP deadline: Law 21/2015 Art 13, 90 days [S0035] (official rule) vs expat guide, 60 days + QR 10/day [S0133].
- In-country change of visa purpose: MOI Decision 25/2019 Art 15(2), discretionary change allowed [S0038] (official rule) vs BAL 2015 alert, conversion from business/arrival/tourist to work not allowed [S0131].

## E: E

## Findings, part E: domestic workers, family passes, long-stay and professional residence

Researched 7 Nov 2026. Ledger rows E0001-E0085 (ledger-parts/E.csv). Main sources: Qatar's Domestic Workers Law No. 15 of 2017 and Permanent Residence Law No. 10 of 2018 (Arabic, Al Meezan, Ministry of Justice, via Firecrawl; both shown "in force"); Ministry of Interior (MOI) service pages (family residence visa, family visit visa, first-time residence permit, complaints against sponsors, absconding reports, visa inquiry) and the MOI Family Visa application form (PDF modified 2 Mar 2026); the MOI permanent-residency page; Qatar News Agency report of the MOI statement on family visas (3 Dec 2023); the Government Communications Office copy of the labour ministry's minimum wage / NOC statement (2020); the Ministry of Labour's National Report on Combating Human Trafficking 2024 and Labour Complaint Form (Wayback copies); the Qatar Tourism instruction manual on non-Qatari property ownership (2023); Bangladesh: Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare housekeeping training policy (sent 26 Aug 2025) and women-domestic-worker agency approval (1 Sep 2025) (both image-only, transcribed), BMET female-employment table (May 2025), Bangladesh Embassy Doha pages, Wage Earners' Welfare Board safe-house page.

Status note: ledger.py forces `possibly-outdated` on any page dated before 7 Nov 2025 or undated. That covers the laws (2017/2018, still "in force" on Al Meezan), all MOI service pages (undated) and the Bangladesh policy documents (Aug/Sep 2025). Only E0046, E0047 (WEWB, Oct 2026) and E0064, E0065 (MOI form, Mar 2026) are `verified`. The Ministry of Labour website (mol.gov.qa, moawen.mol.gov.qa) could not be reached at all, so Moawen facts are news `lead` rows.

Context for the doc: Qatar's law sets the domestic-worker rights; the Bangladesh side adds stricter rules for women (25-45, compulsory one-month course, zero migration cost). Family residence and family visit visas need a non-labour profession and QR 5,000-10,000 salary, so ordinary labourers cannot bring family. Permanent residency (20 years' residence, Arabic, max 100 a year) and property residence (QR 730,000+) are effectively out of reach for workers; no Qatari scheme excludes Bangladeshis by nationality.

### 1. Domestic worker (housemaid, house driver, cook, gardener, nanny)

**Who it covers:** anyone doing household work for a wage under the employer's direction: drivers, nannies, cooks, gardeners and similar [E0001]. Recruitment, medical test and residence permit follow the rules for other migrant workers [E0002].

**Is it open to Bangladeshis?**
- Yes. The Bangladesh Embassy in Doha attests housemaid visas; without that attestation the housemaid gets no clearance from Bangladesh [E0041, E0042].
- BMET counted 2,331 Bangladeshi women going to Qatar in 2024 and 1,172 in Jan-May 2025 (all jobs, not only domestic work) [E0040].
- Qatar's age limit: 18 to 60, both sexes [E0007]. Bangladesh's limit for women domestic workers is stricter: 25-45 (returnees up to 50) [E0030]. No Bangladesh-side age limit for male house drivers was found (Need to know).

**Bangladesh-side rules for women** (Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare policy, 2023 revised, sent Aug 2025):
- One-month (24 working days) compulsory residential housekeeping course at a BMET Technical Training Centre (TTC) [E0028, E0034].
- Apply online after BMET's admission notice, pay the set fee and pick the centre and date [E0029]. Fee amount not stated (set by BMET) (Need to know).
- Class 5 pass (can be relaxed), fit, not pregnant, youngest child 6 or older, no contagious or incurable disease [E0031, E0032].
- Bring proof of class 5, a no-objection letter from the legal guardian, and a copy of birth certificate, NID or passport; originals at the interview; enrol in person [E0033].
- Pass mark 70%; 90% attendance [E0034]. The course covers how to contact the police and the Bangladesh embassy [E0035].
- Certificate downloadable online, valid 2 years; 3-day refresher if 2-3 years old; full course again after 3 years; returnees with 1+ year abroad do the 3-day refresher [E0036].
- Agencies approved to send women domestic workers (21 on 1 Sep 2025) may take **no migration cost** from the woman, must deposit Tk 15 lakh with BMET, sign an undertaking that the licence may be cancelled if she is abused, and employ at least one woman in their office [E0037, E0038, E0039]. The 1 Sep 2025 list is not Qatar-specific.

**Recruitment in Qatar:**
- Through a licensed recruitment office; or the employer directly, with labour department approval [E0006].
- Since Sept 2026, the Ministry of Labour's Moawen platform (moawen.mol.gov.qa) is reported as the government channel for domestic-worker recruitment; fake Moawen websites exist; MOL hotline 16505 (unofficial, not verified) [E0081, E0082].
- The Ministry of Labour published a list of 221 registered recruitment companies (Sept 2026) with a QR code, and tells people to check it before paying (unofficial, not verified) [E0083].

**Contract:** written, certified by the labour ministry's department, 3 copies (one for each side, one filed) [E0003]; in Arabic, a translation may be attached, Arabic prevails [E0004]; must state the work, start date, probation, length, renewal/ending terms and the wage and pay date [E0005]. Paid probation [E0008].

**Pay and conditions (law):**
- Minimum wage QR 1,000 basic + QR 500 housing + QR 300 food unless the employer provides them (Law 17/2020) [E0048].
- Paid monthly in QR by the 3rd of the next month; only into the worker's bank account or in cash against a signed receipt [E0011, E0012].
- No deduction of recruitment costs [E0013].
- Free food, decent housing, health care and medicine [E0009]; dignity, no physical or psychological harm, no work on sick leave or rest days unless agreed [E0010].
- Max 10 hours a day unless otherwise agreed, plus breaks [E0017]; weekly paid rest of 24 consecutive hours [E0018]; 3 weeks' paid annual leave [E0019]; return air ticket every 2 years, one-way ticket on final exit [E0020].
- End-of-service pay at least 3 weeks' wage per year after 1 year [E0021].
- No work outside Qatar without consent [E0014]. The worker may not work for others [E0016].
- Work injury compensation as under the Labour Law [E0025]. Death: employer pays to send the body home if the family asks, deposits all dues within 15 days [E0015].
- Employer fines up to QR 5,000 / QR 10,000 [E0027].

**Ending the job:** the employer may dismiss without notice or that year's end-of-service pay if the worker breaches the law or contract [E0022]. The worker may leave early and keep full end-of-service pay if the employer breaches, lied when hiring, assaults the worker (or a family member does), or ignores a serious danger [E0023]. Claims expire 1 year after the job ends [E0026].

**Changing employer:** the No-Objection Certificate was abolished in 2020 for workers (Decree Laws 18 and 19 of 2020) [E0049]. Whether and how a domestic worker changes employer (notice period, Ministry of Labour e-service) was not confirmed from an official page for domestic workers (Need to know; cross-check part B's change-of-employer SOP).

**Complaints and abuse:**
- Disputes go to the labour dispute settlement committees (Labour Law ch. 11 bis) [E0024]; more committees were added in 2020 [E0050].
- Ministry of Labour Labour Complaint Form: to the Labour Disputes Department; asks if the worker agrees to referral to the Labour Dispute Resolution Committee; attach the Arabic contract and ID [E0055].
- MOL 24/7 hotline 16008 (Arabic, English, interpreters) and a Unified Platform for Complaints and Whistleblowers [E0051]; anti-trafficking hotline 16044 [E0052].
- MOI Search and Follow-up Department takes free complaints against a sponsor for abuse (passport or ID copy + written complaint) [E0056]. Sponsors can file free "absconding" reports against a worker [E0057].
- Shelter in Qatar: the Humanitarian Care House (MOL anti-trafficking committee) shelters trafficking victims with housing, meals, health care, legal support and help home [E0053]; in 2024 it recovered dues for 32 residents through the dispute committee or Labour Complaints Department [E0054].
- Bangladesh side: the Welfare Board's safe houses are in Saudi Arabia, Oman, Jordan, Libya and Lebanon; **Qatar is not listed** [E0046]. A family member in Bangladesh can ask the Welfare Board (directly, through the DC office or DEMO) to write to the embassy; call 16135 [E0047]. Embassy: labour court or the online platform, plus embassy advice [E0044]; hotline +974 33662000 [E0045].
- In practice (Amnesty 2020): of 105 women, 90 worked over 14 hours a day, 87 had passports taken, 13 faced absconding cases after fleeing abuse (unofficial, not verified) [E0084, E0085].

### 2. Family residence (spouse and children of a resident)

- Private-sector sponsor: technical or specialised job and salary QR 10,000, or QR 6,000 with family housing, shown in the employment contract (MOI, Dec 2023) [E0058]. Government sector: employer family housing or QR 10,000 [E0059]. So labour-category workers cannot bring family.
- Children 6-18 must be in a licensed school in Qatar (or enrolled abroad via the education ministry platform) to get or renew a residence permit [E0060].
- MOI Family Residence Visa: fee QAR 200; attested work contract showing profession and salary, attested relationship certificate, good-conduct certificate for adults, medical test outside Qatar; MOI Services Centres [E0062, E0063].
- MOI Application for Family Visa form (2026): host's ID copy, passport copy, certified marriage contract and children's birth certificates, certified contract showing job and salary, police clearance for adults, health insurance for the whole stay, medical exam abroad; form typed in Arabic, originals at registration [E0064, E0065].
- Residence permit fee for accompanying family: QR 400 [E0066].
- Bangladesh Embassy Doha attests a family visa (visa, passports, nikahnama), QR 50 each, 3 working days [E0043].
- Dependants and work: not found in an official source (Need to know). A Fragomen note mentions a service to convert family residence to work residence (lead only, not fetched).

### 3. Family visit visa

- Host: profession outside the labour category, salary QAR 5,000 with an accredited contract, family accommodation in the host's name; visitor needs health insurance for the whole visit [E0069, E0061, E0068].
- Who: parents, spouse, children; siblings, grandparents, grandchildren; nephews/nieces, uncles and aunts [E0070].
- One month, extendable 5 more months for first-degree relatives; fee QR 200, extension QR 200 a month [E0067].
- Apply on Metrash2 with the visitor's passport (6 months valid), proof of relationship and proof of accommodation [E0068]. Track with MOI Visa Inquiry (application number or visa number) [E0071].

### 4. Permanent residency, property residence, investor

- Permanent residency (Law 10/2018): 20 years' continuous legal residence (10 if born in Qatar; up to 60 days a year abroad allowed but deducted), enough income, good conduct, enough Arabic [E0072, E0073]. Max 100 a year [E0074]. Spouse and children under 18 share residence, health and education privileges [E0075]; holder may invest without a Qatari partner in set sectors and own property in set areas [E0076]. Fee set by MOI decision (amount not found) [E0077]. Apply via MOI's PR page (eligibility inquiry, online application) [E0078]. No nationality exclusion.
- Property residence: property over QR 730,000 gives residency; over QR 3.65 million gives PR-type privileges [E0079]. Apply on the Ministry of Justice website; offices at The Pearl and Lusail; 40216669, NQO@moj.gov.qa [E0080].
- Investor/skilled-talent visas: none researched beyond PR Art. 2 categories ("special competences the state needs", in the law text) (Need to know).

### Draft steps

#### Domestic worker (woman)
1. **Check you qualify:** 25-45 (returnee up to 50), class 5 pass, not pregnant, youngest child 6+ [E0030, E0031, E0032]. Qatar's own limit is 18-60 [E0007].
2. **Do the one-month housekeeping course** at a TTC: apply online when BMET opens admission, pay the fee, bring guardian's NOC, class 5 proof and NID/birth certificate/passport; residential; 70% to pass; download the certificate (valid 2 years) [E0028, E0029, E0033, E0034, E0036].
3. **Go only through an agency approved to send women domestic workers**; it may not charge you anything [E0037, E0038]. (Check the agency's licence on OEP: see part A SOP.)
4. **Read the contract** before signing: it must be certified by Qatar's labour ministry, in Arabic with a translation you understand; check the wage (at least QR 1,000 plus food and housing), weekly day off, hours [E0003, E0004, E0005, E0048, E0018].
5. **The sponsor gets the visa attested** at the Bangladesh Embassy Doha through an enlisted agent (QR 50); without it BMET will not clear you [E0041, E0042].
6. **Medical, BMET clearance, smart card** as for other workers (part A/C).
7. **In Qatar:** keep your contract copy; insist on pay by the 3rd of each month into your bank account or against a signed receipt [E0011, E0012].

#### Domestic worker (man: house driver, cook, gardener)
- Same Qatar rules [E0001-E0027]; no Bangladesh course; age and licence rules for drivers not found (Need to know).

#### Bring your family to live in Qatar (resident with a qualifying job)
1. Check you qualify: non-labour (technical/specialised) job and QR 10,000 salary, or QR 6,000 + family housing in your contract [E0058].
2. Get your marriage certificate and children's birth certificates certified (attestation route in Bangladesh/Qatar embassy: Need to know) [E0064].
3. Get your employment contract certified showing job and salary [E0063, E0064].
4. Fill the MOI Family Visa form in Arabic, names as in passports; apply at an MOI Services Centre (or Metrash2: Need to know) with host ID, passports, certificates, police clearance for adults, health insurance; pay QAR 200 [E0062, E0064, E0065].
5. Family does the medical test outside Qatar [E0063].
6. After arrival: residence permit (QR 400) [E0066]; enrol children 6-18 in school [E0060]. Optional: embassy attestation of the family visa, QR 50 [E0043].

#### Invite family on a visit visa
1. Check: non-labour profession, QAR 5,000 salary, family accommodation in your name [E0069].
2. On Metrash2: upload the visitor's passport (6 months valid), proof of relationship, proof of accommodation; arrange health insurance [E0068, E0069].
3. Pay QR 200; visa for 1 month; extend up to 5 months (first-degree) at QR 200 a month [E0067].
4. Check status on MOI Visa Inquiry with the application or visa number [E0071].

### Draft SOPs

**SOP: Check a domestic-worker recruitment office in Qatar is licensed**
- When: before paying or signing with any office or advert offering a Qatar housemaid/driver job.
- Steps: check the Ministry of Labour's list of registered recruitment companies (221 in Sept 2026; reached by QR code / mol.gov.qa) and use only moawen.mol.gov.qa [E0083, E0082]. Bangladesh side: the agency must be BMET-licensed and, for women, on the ministry's approved list [E0037].
- What you see: Need to know (list and Moawen not reachable; blocked).
- If it goes wrong: MOL hotline 16505 (reported) or 16008 [E0082, E0051].

**SOP: What a domestic worker does if not paid or abused in Qatar**
- When: wages late (after the 3rd), no day off, passport taken, beaten or threatened.
- Steps: (1) In danger: call the police (number: part B) or the anti-trafficking line 16044 [E0052]. (2) Call MOL's 24/7 line 16008 (Arabic/English, interpreters) [E0051]. (3) File a labour complaint: MOL Labour Complaint Form with a copy of your Arabic contract and ID; say whether you agree to referral to the Labour Dispute Resolution Committee [E0055]. (4) Abuse by sponsor: free complaint at MOI Search and Follow-up Department with passport/ID copy and written complaint [E0056]. (5) Call the Bangladesh Embassy hotline +974 33662000 [E0045]. (6) Family in Bangladesh: apply to the Welfare Board (16135) to ask the embassy to act [E0047].
- What you get: dispute committee decision; trafficking victims may be sheltered in the Humanitarian Care House [E0053, E0054]. There is no Bangladesh safe house in Qatar [E0046].
- If it goes wrong: claims expire 1 year after you leave the job, so complain early [E0026]. Leaving without complaining can lead to an absconding report [E0057, E0085].

**SOP: Bring your family on residence** (see Draft steps above; links: MOI Family Residence Visa page, Family Visa form PDF) [E0062-E0066].

**SOP: Invite family on a visit visa** (Metrash2; MOI Visa Inquiry fees2.moi.gov.qa/moipay/visa/inquiry) [E0067-E0071].

**SOP: Check a family/visit visa is real**
- Steps: MOI Visa Inquiry (portal.moi.gov.qa > Inquiries > Visa Services) with the visa number or application number; print the visa [E0071].
- What you see / statuses: Need to know (form not tested).

### Scams
- **Fake Moawen websites / fake recruitment adverts:** sites copying Moawen and social-media adverts by unlicensed offices; check the MOL list and the official address moawen.mol.gov.qa (unofficial, not verified) [E0082, E0083].
- **Agencies charging women domestic workers:** approved agencies may take no migration cost [E0038].
- **Family visa sellers:** a labourer cannot sponsor family on residence (salary/occupation rules) [E0058, E0069]; anyone selling a "family visa" to a labour-category worker should be treated as suspect. No official warning found (Need to know).

### Need to know
- Moawen: official steps, fees, worker access (MOL site unreachable; only news) [E0081].
- Current MOL list of licensed recruitment offices (Wayback 2025 copy damaged).
- Bangladesh: fee for the housekeeping course; any Bangladesh rule for male house drivers (age, test); whether Qatar is open for women domestic workers today (searched BMET notices and miscellaneous pages in English/Bangla: no Qatar-specific notice found).
- Domestic workers changing employer: notice and MOL procedure for domestic workers specifically.
- Domestic workers' minimum hours exceptions ("unless otherwise agreed") and probation length (ministerial decision not found).
- Family residence: whether Metrash2 is the channel (QNA says all services on Metrash2 and MOI website [E0061]); current salary rule after Dec 2023; whether dependants can work; attestation route for Bangladeshi certificates (Qatar embassy Dhaka page blocked; Qatar not an Apostille party per HCCH table).
- Family visit visa: whether QR 200 covers all relatives; insurance cost.
- Permanent residency fee and card validity (set by MOI decision; not found); property law articles (Al Meezan didn't load) and the 2026 cabinet resolution on property areas.
- Skilled-talent/investor visas other than PR and property residence.
- Embassy page dates are 2021 (housemaid attestation, counselling): current fee may differ.

### Conflicts
- None between official sources found. Qatar's age limit (18-60) [E0007] and Bangladesh's limit for women (25-45) [E0030] both apply; Bangladesh's is stricter.
- Law vs practice: 10-hour day and weekly day off in law [E0017, E0018] vs Amnesty's finding of 14+ hour days and no days off (2020) [E0084]. Not an official conflict; shown as practice.

### Blocked
See blocked-parts/E.md: moawen.mol.gov.qa, mol.gov.qa (recruitment-office list, warning), Hukoomi family pages, MOI PR eligibility inquiry/apply tabs, Al Meezan property-law articles, Qatar embassy Dhaka attestation, ministry Facebook posts.

## M: manual files and user-supplied pages (lead, 7 Nov 2026)

- QVC Bangladesh pages (user browser saves): address, hours, steps, medical steps, security rules, documents (passport more than 195 days), lounge fee BDT 4,260 (optional, paid by sponsor if booked online), tracking statuses [M0001-M0042].
- Hukoomi: "Issue Residence Permit Application through Metrash" (free; steps) [M0043-M0047].
- Gulftrick Metrash guide (30 Oct 2026), unofficial: registration steps, Metrash2 replaced [M0048-M0058].
- Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare Facebook post, 18 May 2026, 7th Joint Committee [M0059-M0069].

### Resolutions
- W0009 (news lead: one Qatar visa and medical centre, in Dhaka; request for 8 divisional cities) -> official M0063, M0064.
- QVC Sylhet: QVC's own pages list Sylhet and say it accepts appointments [M0013, M0014]; the Ministry said on 18 May 2026 there is only one centre, in Dhaka [M0063]. Official vs official: show both.
- 107,598 workers: the Ministry post prints the year as 2023 [M0062]; BMET's data give 107,598 for 2025 [W0001]. Use W0001 for the 2025 figure.

## A: A

## Findings, part A: practical pass, recruitment and real costs (Bangladesh to Qatar)

Researcher A, 7 November 2026. Ledger: `ledger-parts/A.csv` (A0001-A0045). Blocked: `blocked-parts/A.md`. This part adds what happens in practice (status `unofficial` / `lead`) and the official Bangladesh law on dalals, visa trading and fraud that the topic parts had not yet logged. It cites other parts' ids where they already hold the fact (W, B, E, L, M, F, H, V), so nothing is logged twice.

Terms to explain inline in the doc: **dalal** = unlicensed broker/middleman, often a relative or neighbour; **sub-agent / representative (প্রতিনিধি)** = a broker who, since the 2023 amendment, must be registered and appointed by a licensed agency; **visa trading / "free visa"** = buying a visa from a Qatari employer or middleman and reselling it; **VRN** = visa reference number used to book at QVC; **RL number** = Bangladesh recruiting-agency licence number; **OEP** = BMET's Overseas Employment Platform; **QVC** = Qatar Visa Center (Dhaka, run for Qatar's Ministry of Interior); **MOL / MOI / MOCI** = Qatar's Ministries of Labour, Interior, Commerce and Industry.

---

### 1. Recruitment and costs

#### 1.1 The official channel (how a real Qatar job reaches a worker)
- OEP's own map of the process: the Qatari employer registers and submits the job demand; the Bangladesh mission (Embassy Doha labour wing) verifies the employer and attests the demand and visa; the worker registers with BMET by NID; a licensed recruiting agency selects workers from the skill database; then PDO, medical, visa, emigration clearance and the EC (smart) card [A0029, A0030]. Embassy attestation of the demand letter needs the agent agreement, the Qatar visa approval letter and company details [W0046, W0047].
- Qatar side: an employer may recruit abroad only through an MOL-licensed recruiter or itself with MOL approval [W0016]; a licensed recruiter may not charge the worker any fee or cost [W0019]; visas may not be sold or transferred [W0032].
- Bangladesh side: the government may fix a maximum migration cost by order (Act 2013 s.21) [A0018].
- No G2G/BOESL channel for Qatar (part W, W0006). Qatar's 2026 interest in electricians, plumbers, AC technicians and welders trained at five TTCs is news only [W0007, W0008].

#### 1.2 How workers actually find Qatar jobs (practice)
- Most migrants pay a broker: BBS's 2023 socio-economic survey (via TBS) found 52.03% paid their migration costs to brokers, 42.09% to private agencies, 3.09% to government bodies [A0001, A0002] (unofficial, not verified). Brokers are often friends and relatives, because people don't trust strangers with large sums [A0045].
- 58.24% borrowed to pay [A0003] (unofficial).
- "Free visa": an OKUP 2025 study (via Dhaka Tribune) found 51% of 1,084 migrants went on so-called free visas, which cost more than regular work visas and add work-permit and job-search costs at destination; for Saudi Arabia, Oman, Qatar and Dubai workers pay three to four times official rates [A0009, A0008] (unofficial). Qatar law: there is no lawful "free visa"; trading visas is a crime in Qatar [W0032, W0033] and in Bangladesh (Act 2013 s.33: 2-7 years and at least Tk 3 lakh) [A0022]. Embassy Doha: free-visa workers have no legal help if unpaid [W0080-W0082].
- How visa trading works: Qatari employers or agents demand USD 300-500 per visa from Bangladeshi agents, passed to the worker [W0098]; some agencies buy each visa for QR 5,000-6,000 while others get them free [W0099]; payment is "usually no transaction through banking channels" [A0013]; TIB estimated visas were bought in 90% of cases (2016) [A0014]. All unofficial.
- **Sub-agents are now legal only if registered.** Act 2023: a sub-agent/representative is a person registered under s.14A who collects workers for an agency [A0025]; an agency may appoint only a registered person [A0026]; acting as a sub-agent without registration: 1 year or Tk 50,000-2 lakh [A0027]; agency appointing one in breach: 1 year or Tk 1-5 lakh [A0028]. **Need to know:** the registration rules and any public list of registered sub-agents, so a worker can check his dalal (searched bdlaws, probashi.gov.bd, BMET, OEP; only the Act and ILO news that the change was endorsed Nov 2024).

#### 1.3 What workers really pay (vs the rules)
- **Rule:** licensed recruiters in Qatar may not take any money from the worker [W0019]; in the one BMET Qatar permission letter (women domestic workers, 2025) the employer bears all costs to arrival [W0040, W0043]; women domestic workers: approved agencies may take no migration cost [E0038].
- **Official maximum for Qatar: not found.** Reported: Tk 100,780 (FE Jan 2018 [W0104]; Dhaka Tribune Dec 2025 [A0006]); a district employment officer said Tk 1.15-1.2 lakh for six Gulf countries incl. Qatar (bdnews24, July 2025) [A0011, lead]. Both news; see Conflicts.
- **Practice figures (all unofficial, not verified):**
  - BBS 2020 cost survey: Qatar Tk 4.02 lakh average [B0170]; all destinations Tk 416,789 vs monthly income Tk 23,693 [A0005].
  - Official statistic (Bangladesh SDG tracker, BBS 2020): recruitment cost for Qatar = 18.1% of a year's income earned in Qatar (17.6% all destinations) [A0004, official, destination-section statistic].
  - Guardian (2021): Bangladeshis typically paid USD 3,000-4,000 for Qatar jobs [W0103]; USD 1.5-2 bn in 2011-2020 [W0100].
  - Worker reviews (2023): Tk 300,000, 350,000, 400,000, 420,000 for Qatar construction/garment/electrician jobs [A0015-A0017]; a 2016 case Tk 360,000 [W0102]; Tk 7 lakh (2016) [H0014].
  - Low-skilled workers to Saudi, Kuwait, Qatar, Singapore pay Tk 4-10 lakh (TBS Dec 2025) [B0169].
  - About 78% of the cost goes to middlemen and sub-agents at home and abroad [A0007]; costs on top of official fees include visa, work permit, medical, training, airfare, tax, welfare fee, agency charge, insurance [A0008].
  - HRW 2026: illegal recruitment fees still common and largely unpunished [F0022].
- ILO-backed exception: the 2018-2021 Bangladesh-Qatar construction pilot cut average cost 92%; 93% of workers had no debt [W0014, W0015].
- **Need to know:** a current (2024-2026) Qatar-specific cost figure from an official survey (BBS 2020 report and SEDS 2023 PDFs not found; blocked list).

#### 1.4 How long it takes
- **Need to know:** typical time from demand letter to departure for Qatar (searched English/Bangla news, QVC pages, forums; only blogs and expat forums on QVC medical result times, not logged). QVC: completing biometrics/medical does not mean a visa is issued; the employer receives the result [M0035, M0036].

#### 1.5 What goes wrong after paying (practice)
- Fake contract / different job: workers report fake job-contract letters and not getting the promised job or pay [A0015, A0016, A0017]; welder promised QR 2,500 put on a farm at QR 800 [W0102]. QVC contract e-signing may have cut substitution but not fees [W0101].
- Unpaid wages after arrival: labour-supply companies delay wages (HRW Dec 2025) [F0027]; WPS/WSIF not effective (HRW) [L0203]; Q1 2026 MOL complaint numbers [L0201].
- Agencies in Qatar: MOL closed 23 recruitment offices in 2023 for breaking licensing rules and ignoring complaints [A0032].
- Official defences: Bangladesh Act 2013 s.31 makes taking money on a false job promise, withholding passport/visa, or luring with false wage promises a crime (up to 5 years + at least Tk 1 lakh) [A0019, A0020]; fraud victims have a right to legal aid [A0024]; complaint routes in part B [B0016-B0021, B0031].

---

### 2. Scams and how to avoid them

| Scam | How to spot it | Official warning / check | Ids |
|---|---|---|---|
| "Free visa" / buying a visa | Offer of a visa "without a company", or price for the visa itself | No free visa exists (Ministry, via news) [W0003, B0165]; visa trade illegal in Qatar [W0032, W0033] and Bangladesh [A0022]; Embassy FAQ [W0080] | SOP A2, A3 |
| Unlicensed dalal / sub-agent | Not from an agency office; can't show agency appointment | Sub-agent must be registered [A0025-A0027]; agency licence on OEP [W0087, A0031] | SOP A1 |
| Agency with cancelled licence | OEP shows "Cancelled"/"Suspended" though the date is still in future | Recruiting after cancellation is an offence [A0023]; status column [A0031] | SOP A1 |
| Fake job advert (Facebook etc.) | Ads not approved by BMET; pay first | Ads without government/BMET approval illegal [A0021]; Qatar MOL: unlicensed offices post convincing social-media ads, check the licensed list [A0035, A0036, A0037] (lead) | SOP A1, A3 |
| Fake QVC agent / paid QVC appointment / "medical fixer" | Someone sells slots, asks for VRN on WhatsApp, promises "fit" result | QVC offers no jobs [W0053]; appointments free, book only on qatarvisacenter.com with VRN [A0043]; never share VRN/passport no. on WhatsApp/phone [A0040]; QVC staff take no decisions [A0041]; lounge doesn't guarantee approval [A0042] (all lead except W0053) | SOP A4 |
| Fake visa / visa not issued | Paper "visa" only; no record on MOI | MOI Visa Inquiry [W0063, W0064, V0057, V0059]; QVC Track [M0034]; BMET visa check [B0014, B0015, B0011] | SOP A2 |
| Fake employer / company not real | No CR or establishment ID | MOL approval needs active establishment [W0024]; MOCI Company Inquiry Service [A0044, lead; blocked] | SOP A3 |
| Fake Moawen / fake MOL sites and accounts (domestic work) | Site not ending mol.gov.qa | Moawen only at moawen.mol.gov.qa [E0082]; MOL services only on mol.gov.qa platforms [F0013]; fake MOL accounts collect ID data [A0038]; MOI: avoid links claiming to be government [A0039] (all lead) | E's SOP |
| Hayya/visit visa "with job" | Visit or Hayya visa offered for work | Part V [V0128, V0024] | SOP A2 |
| Contract substitution | Contract at QVC differs from promise | Read and keep the QVC contract [W0090]; check on MOL portal after arrival [W0070] | SOP A3 |

---

### 3. Draft SOPs (do-it-yourself)

**SOP A1: Check that an agency (and its dalal) is allowed to recruit you**
- When: before you give any agency, broker or relative your passport or money.
- Steps:
  1. Ask the person for the agency's name and RL number. If he is not agency staff, ask for proof that the agency appointed him as a registered sub-agent/representative; an unregistered sub-agent commits an offence [A0025-A0027]. **Need to know:** where the sub-agent register can be checked.
  2. Open https://www.oep.gov.bd/agencies (free); type the RL number or name in the search box [W0087].
  3. Read the row: licence number, name, address, office phone, **License Status**, validity date [W0087].
  4. Trust the status, not the date: a licence can show "Cancelled" with a validity date years ahead (e.g. RL0345, Cancelled, 31/Dec/2028) [A0031]; recruiting after cancellation is a crime [A0023].
  5. Call the office phone shown on OEP, not the broker's number, and visit the office address (generic; part W SOP W1).
- What you see: Active / Expired / Cancelled / Suspended (7 Nov 2026: 2,205 / 264 / 235 / 151 of 2,863) [W0087].
- If it goes wrong: complaint to BMET (DG, with proof, free, 30 working days) [B0016, B0017]; Ministry enforcement email sasenforcement@probashi.gov.bd [B0020]; Ministry complaint cell minister.expat@gmail.com [B0031]. Give who took money, from whom, when and how much [A0012]. OEP hotline 09614445271 [B0003].
- Note for Qatar domestic work: also check the Qatari recruitment office on MOL's licensed list [W0105, E0083, A0036] (list blocked; see blocked-parts/A.md about the manual file).

**SOP A2: Check that your Qatar visa is real (before paying the rest)**
- When: the agency says "your visa has come" or shows a visa copy.
- Steps:
  1. MOI Visa Inquiry: https://fees2.moi.gov.qa/moipay/visa/inquiry?language=en (MOI > Inquiries > Visa Services) [W0062, V0057]. Choose search by visa number or passport number, pick nationality Bangladesh, type the image code, press Inquire [V0059, W0064].
  2. Check the visa type is a work visa (not visit/Hayya) [V0024, V0128] and the employer and job match what you were promised (**Need to know:** what fields a genuine result shows).
  3. After your QVC visit: QVC "Track Application" with passport number + visa number + captcha [M0034]; statuses Appointment Confirmed, Employment Contract Status, Biometric Enrolment Complete, Medical Test Complete, Medical Test Status, Results Available with Employer [M0042].
  4. Bangladesh side: BMET checks job visas and demand letters free in 3 working days; Qatar visas are checked online; bring the visa and the passport page with the visa stamp; or go to the district manpower office [B0014, B0015, B0011]. **Need to know:** the URL/app BMET uses for the online check (not found on bmet.gov.bd or OEP).
- What it means: a rejected application shows a Remarks icon with reasons [W0063]; finishing biometrics/medical does not mean a visa is issued [M0035].
- If it goes wrong: no record = do not pay; complain (SOP A1 routes).

**SOP A3: Check the job offer and the employer**
- When: before signing anything or paying.
- Steps:
  1. Ask for the demand letter attested by the Bangladesh Embassy Doha and the Qatar visa approval letter it was attested with [W0047] (the mission verifies the employer [A0029]).
  2. Ask for the company's name, establishment ID and CR number; MOL issues recruitment approval only for an active, unbanned establishment [W0024]. MOCI's Company Inquiry Service (ucs.moci.gov.qa) shows a company's regulatory record [A0044, lead]. **Need to know:** whether it is free and open without a Qatari login (page blocked).
  3. Get job, wage and terms in writing; the contract must state them [W0073]; compare with the minimum wage QR 1,000 + 500 + 300 [L0074].
  4. At QVC read the contract on screen before e-signing and keep your copy [W0090]; after arrival download it from MOL's contract portal [W0070].
- If wrong: a false wage promise is a crime in Bangladesh [A0019]; complain (SOP A1).

**SOP A4: Go to QVC without a middleman**
- Steps: the appointment is booked on qatarvisacenter.com with your VRN; appointments are free and the employer pays for the visa (QVC alert, lead) [A0043]; the biometric visa fee goes to Qatar's MOI, not SQ Services [M0024]; the lounge is optional, sponsor-paid when booked online, and does not speed up or guarantee the visa [M0022, M0025, W0060, A0042]. Never share your VRN or passport number with "QVC" callers on WhatsApp [A0040]. Medical results are decided by Qatar's health ministry, not QVC staff [A0041].
- Help: QVC Dhaka +880 9666 777 101, info.bgd@qatarvisacenter.com [M0001, M0002].

**SOP A5: Pay safely and keep proof**
- Pay the agency only into its bank account and keep receipts [B0009]; don't use middlemen [B0019]. Traded visas are typically paid in cash outside banks [A0013], which leaves no proof.
- Keep: names, dates, amounts, who received money; the Ministry acts on exactly these [A0012].
- Legal aid is a right for fraud victims [A0024]; Act 2013 penalties [A0019-A0022].
